R01 Thermodynamic Stability

Energy above the convex hull retrieved from Materials Project metadata.

Reasons citing low or negative formation energy, or low energy above the convex hull, as indicators of a material’s energetic favorability and synthesizability. This includes explicit references to phase stability against decomposition and proximity to the ground state.

def rule_01_thermodynamic_stability(structure, mp_id=None):
    if mp_id is None:
        return 0.0
    doc_data = _summary_doc(mp_id)
    if doc_data:
        if "energy_above_hull" in doc_data and doc_data["energy_above_hull"] is not None:
            return float(doc_data["energy_above_hull"])
        print(f"[WARN] 'energy_above_hull' not found or is None for {mp_id}")
    else:
        print(f"[WARN] No summary found for {mp_id}")

    return 0.0
R02 Phonon Stability Proxy

Heuristic proxy computed from mean coordination number and density.

Reasons referencing the absence of imaginary phonon modes, positive phonon frequencies, or lack of soft vibrational modes as essential for dynamic and vibrational stability, which is necessary for successful synthesis.

def rule_02_phonon_stability_proxy(structure):
    vnn = VoronoiNN()
    mean_cn = np.mean([vnn.get_cn(structure, i) for i in range(len(structure))])
    mass = sum(site.specie.atomic_mass for site in structure.sites)
    volume = structure.lattice.volume
    density = mass / volume
    return float(mean_cn * density)
R03 Coordination Environment

Mean coordination number estimated using CrystalNN.

Reasons emphasizing typical, common, or chemically plausible atomic coordination numbers and geometries for constituent atoms, supporting stable bonding and ease of synthesis.

def rule_03_avg_coordination_number(structure):
    try:
        cnn = CrystalNN()
        cn_list = [cnn.get_cn(structure, i) for i in range(len(structure))]
        return float(np.mean(cn_list))
    except:
        return 0.0
R04 Space Group Symmetry

Crystallographic space group number computed using SpacegroupAnalyzer.

Reasons highlighting high, common, or simple symmetry and space groups as facilitating easier synthesis, structural stability, and crystallization.

def rule_04_space_group_symmetry(structure):
    try:
        return float(SpacegroupAnalyzer(structure).get_space_group_number())
    except:
        return 0.0
R05 Packing Density

Crystal density computed from the structure.

Reasons discussing realistic, moderate, or physically plausible density and efficient atomic packing as supporting structural stability and synthesizability.

def rule_05_packing_density(structure):
    return float(structure.density)
R06 Band Gap (MP)

Band gap retrieved from Materials Project metadata.

Reasons focusing on appropriate, moderate, or reasonable band gap values and electronic structure (e.g., non-metallic, absence of unstable states) as factors in material stability and synthesis.

def rule_06_band_gap(structure, mp_id=None):
    if mp_id is None:
        return 0.0
    doc_data = _summary_doc(mp_id)
    if doc_data and doc_data.get("band_gap") is not None:
        return float(doc_data["band_gap"])
    return 0.0
R07 Bond Length/Angle Deviation

Deviation of local bond lengths and angles from typical reference values.

Reasons referencing realistic, typical, or chemically reasonable bond lengths and angles, and the avoidance of highly strained, short, or long bonds, as indicators of structural stability and synthesizability.

def rule_07_bond_length_angle_deviation(structure):
    cnn = CrystalNN()
    bond_lengths = []
    bond_angles = []
    for i, site in enumerate(structure):
        try:
            neighs = cnn.get_nn_info(structure, i)
        except:
            continue
        coords = [n['site'].coords for n in neighs]
        if not coords:
            continue
        for c in coords:
            bond_lengths.append(np.linalg.norm(site.coords - c))
        if len(coords) >= 2:
            for j in range(len(coords)):
                for k in range(j+1, len(coords)):
                    v1 = coords[j] - site.coords
                    v2 = coords[k] - site.coords
                    cos_angle = np.dot(v1, v2) / (np.linalg.norm(v1) * np.linalg.norm(v2))
                    angle = np.arccos(np.clip(cos_angle, -1, 1)) * 180 / np.pi
                    bond_angles.append(angle)
    typical_bond_length = 2.0
    typical_bond_angle = 109.5
    if bond_lengths and bond_angles:
        length_dev = np.mean([abs(l - typical_bond_length) for l in bond_lengths])
        angle_dev = np.mean([abs(a - typical_bond_angle) for a in bond_angles])
        return float(length_dev + 0.1 * angle_dev)
    else:
        return 0.0
R08 Oxidation State Consistency

Bond-valence-based assessment of oxidation-state and charge consistency.

Reasons citing the presence of common, stable, or chemically reasonable oxidation states and overall charge neutrality or balanced charge distribution as necessary for stable synthesis.

def rule_08_oxidation_state_consistency(structure):
    comp = structure.composition
    try:
        oxi_states = BVAnalyzer().get_oxi_state_decorated_structure(structure)
        total_charge = sum(site.specie.oxi_state for site in oxi_states.sites)
        return abs(float(total_charge))
    except:
        try:
            total_charge = 0.0
            for el, amt in comp.items():
                common_oxis = Element(el).oxidation_states
                if common_oxis:
                    oxi = max(set(common_oxis), key=common_oxis.count)
                    total_charge += oxi * amt
            return abs(float(total_charge))
        except:
            return 0.0
R09 Lattice Strain

Deviation among lattice parameters as a proxy for lattice distortion or strain.

Reasons mentioning reasonable, moderate, or physically plausible lattice parameters, minimal lattice strain, distortion, or mismatch as beneficial for crystal growth and stability.

def rule_09_lattice_strain(structure):
    a, b, c = structure.lattice.abc
    mean_abc = np.mean([a, b, c])
    strain = np.sqrt(((a - mean_abc) ** 2 + (b - mean_abc) ** 2 + (c - mean_abc) ** 2) / 3) / mean_abc
    return float(strain)
R10 Prototype Similarity Score

Structural similarity to known Materials Project structures using structure matching.

Reasons referencing similarity to experimentally realized, database-known, or prototype structures and motifs as increasing the likelihood of successful synthesis.

def rule_10_prototype_similarity_score(structure):
    """
    Fast, continuous prototype similarity score.
    Only compares to a small sample of database-known structures for speed.
    """
    elements = tuple(sorted(el.symbol for el in structure.composition.elements))
    db_structs = _prototype_structures(elements)

    if not db_structs:
        return 0.0

    matcher = StructureMatcher(ltol=0.2, stol=0.3, angle_tol=5)
    min_dist = np.inf
    for db_struct in db_structs:
        try:
            if len(structure) == len(db_struct):
                dists = [
                    np.linalg.norm(pbc_shortest_vectors(
                        structure.lattice, [structure.frac_coords[i]], [db_struct.frac_coords[i]])[0][0])
                    for i in range(len(structure))
                ]
                mean_dist = np.mean(dists)
                if mean_dist < min_dist:
                    min_dist = mean_dist
        except Exception:
            continue

    if np.isfinite(min_dist):
        return float(np.exp(-min_dist))
    else:
        return 0.0
R11 Elemental Abundance Score

Composition-weighted elemental abundance score based on Clarke abundance.

Reasons emphasizing the use of abundant, non-toxic, non-volatile, and chemically compatible elements, as well as avoidance of rare, hazardous, or highly reactive elements, to improve practical and safe synthesis.

def rule_11_elemental_abundance_score(structure):
    clarke_abundance = {
        "O": 4.6e5, "Si": 2.8e5, "Al": 8.2e4, "Fe": 5.6e4, "Ca": 4.1e4, "Na": 2.3e4, "K": 2.1e4, "Mg": 2.3e4,
        "Ti": 5.6e3, "H": 1.4e4, "P": 1.1e3, "Mn": 1e3, "F": 5.8e2, "Ba": 4.3e2, "S": 3.5e2, "C": 2e2, "Sr": 3.7e2,
        "Zr": 1.6e2, "V": 1.2e2, "Cr": 1.0e2, "Ni": 8.4e1, "Cu": 6.0e1, "Zn": 7.9e1, "Rb": 9.0e0, "Pb": 1.4e1,
        "Li": 2.0e1, "B": 1.0e1, "Sn": 2.2e1, "Co": 2.5e1, "Ce": 6.6e1, "Y": 2.8e1, "Nb": 2.0e1, "La": 3.9e1,
        "Nd": 2.0e1, "U": 2.7e0, "Th": 9.6e0, "Ag": 7.5e-1, "Cd": 1.1e0, "In": 2.5e-1, "Ga": 1.9e0, "Ge": 1.5e0,
        "Mo": 1.2e0, "W": 1.3e0, "Sb": 2.0e0, "Bi": 1.2e0, "Au": 4.0e-2, "Hg": 8.5e-1, "Pt": 3.7e-2, "Ta": 2.0e-1,
        "Re": 4.0e-2, "Os": 5.0e-2, "Ir": 1.0e-2, "Rh": 7.0e-2, "Pd": 1.5e-1, "Sc": 2.2e1, "Tl": 6.0e-1, "Te": 1.0e0,
        "Se": 5.0e-1, "As": 1.8e1, "Br": 2.3e1, "Cl": 1.3e2, "I": 4.5e0, "Cs": 3.0e0
    }
    comp = structure.composition.fractional_composition
    scores = []
    for el, amt in comp.items():
        ab = clarke_abundance.get(el.symbol, 1e-3)
        scores.append(np.log10(ab) * amt)
    return float(np.sum(scores))
R12 Volume Per Atom

Unit-cell volume divided by the number of atomic sites.

Reasons referencing reasonable, moderate, or physically plausible atomic or unit cell volumes as supporting realistic packing and synthesizability.

def rule_12_volume_per_atom(structure):
    return structure.volume / structure.num_sites
R13 Charge Neutrality Deviation

Deviation from total charge neutrality estimated from bond-valence analysis.

Reasons specifically referencing overall charge neutrality or balanced charge distribution as essential for stable synthesis, distinct from general oxidation state balance.

def rule_13_charge_neutrality_deviation(structure):
    comp = structure.composition
    try:
        oxi_struct = BVAnalyzer().get_oxi_state_decorated_structure(structure)
        total_charge = sum(site.specie.oxi_state for site in oxi_struct.sites)
        return abs(float(total_charge))
    except Exception:
        # Estimate net charge from tabulated common oxidation states.
        oxi_table = {"O": -2, "N": -3, "F": -1, "Cl": -1, "Br": -1, "I": -1, "S": -2, "Se": -2,
                     "Li": 1, "Na": 1, "K": 1, "Mg": 2, "Ca": 2, "Al": 3, "Si": 4, "P": 5, "Fe": 3, "Cu": 2, "Zn": 2}
        charge = 0.0
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            charge += oxi_table.get(el.symbol, 0) * amt
        return abs(float(charge))
R14 Bond Valence Sum Deviation

Mean deviation between expected and estimated bond valence sums.

Reasons mentioning bond valence sums close to ideal or expected oxidation states as indicators of stable bonding environments and chemical plausibility.

def rule_14_bond_valence_sum_deviation(structure):
    try:
        bv_struct = BVAnalyzer().get_oxi_state_decorated_structure(structure)
        deviations = []
        for site in bv_struct.sites:
            expected = site.specie.oxi_state
            try:
                actual = BVAnalyzer().get_bv_sum(site, bv_struct)
                deviations.append(abs(actual - expected))
            except Exception:
                continue
        return float(np.mean(deviations)) if deviations else 0.0
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R15 Max Bond Angle Strain

Maximum deviation of local bond angles from idealized coordination angles.

Reasons emphasizing the lack of highly strained, short, or long bonds, distorted polyhedra, or unstable/unusual structural motifs as important for stability and synthesis.

def rule_15_max_bond_angle_strain(structure):
    cnn = CrystalNN()
    angle_strains = []
    for i, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
        try:
            neighbors = cnn.get_nn_info(structure, i)
            if len(neighbors) < 2:
                continue
            neighbor_coords = [n['site'].coords for n in neighbors]
            center = site.coords
            for j in range(len(neighbor_coords)):
                for k in range(j + 1, len(neighbor_coords)):
                    v1 = neighbor_coords[j] - center
                    v2 = neighbor_coords[k] - center
                    cos_angle = np.dot(v1, v2) / (np.linalg.norm(v1) * np.linalg.norm(v2))
                    angle = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip(cos_angle, -1, 1)))
                    ideal_angles = [90, 109.5, 120, 180]
                    min_strain = min(abs(angle - ia) for ia in ideal_angles)
                    angle_strains.append(min_strain)
        except:
            continue
    return float(max(angle_strains)) if angle_strains else 0.0
R16 Mechanical Stability Proxy

Heuristic proxy based on density normalized by average atomic number.

Reasons referencing positive, reasonable elastic constants, mechanical robustness, or low internal stress as supporting structural and synthetic stability.

def rule_16_mechanical_stability_proxy(structure):
    comp = structure.composition
    atomic_numbers = [el.Z for el in comp.elements]
    avg_atomic_number = sum(atomic_numbers[i] * comp[el] for i, el in enumerate(comp.elements)) / comp.num_atoms
    density = structure.density
    return float(density / avg_atomic_number)
R17 Elemental Toxicity/Volatility

Fraction or weighted contribution of toxic, volatile, or highly reactive elements.

Reasons emphasizing the lack of volatile, toxic, highly reactive, or hazardous elements as improving safety and feasibility of synthesis.

def rule_17_elemental_toxicity_volatility(structure):
    toxic_elements = {"Hg", "Cd", "Pb", "As", "Tl", "Se", "Sb", "Be", "Cr", "F", "Cl", "Br", "I", "U", "Th", "Ra"}
    comp = structure.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
    total = sum(comp.values())
    toxic_amt = sum(amt for el, amt in comp.items() if el in toxic_elements)
    return toxic_amt / total if total > 0 else 0.0
R18 Synthesis Temperature Estimate

Composition-weighted average of elemental melting points.

Reasons referencing compatibility with known or practical synthesis conditions (temperature, pressure, atmosphere) and the absence of extreme or highly unusual synthesis requirements.

def rule_18_synthesis_temperature_estimate(structure):
    mp_melting_points = {
        "Li": 453.65, "Na": 370.87, "K": 336.53, "Rb": 312.46, "Cs": 301.59,
        "Mg": 923, "Ca": 1115, "Sr": 1050, "Ba": 1000,
        "Al": 933.47, "Ga": 302.91, "In": 429.75, "Tl": 577,
        "Si": 1687, "Ge": 1211.4, "Sn": 505.08, "Pb": 600.61,
        "B": 2349, "C": 3823, "N": 63.15, "O": 54.36, "F": 53.53, "P": 317.3, "S": 388.36, "Cl": 171.6, "Br": 265.8, "I": 386.85,
        "Ti": 1941, "V": 2183, "Cr": 2180, "Mn": 1519, "Fe": 1811, "Co": 1768, "Ni": 1728, "Cu": 1357.77, "Zn": 692.68,
        "Zr": 2128, "Nb": 2750, "Mo": 2896, "Ru": 2607, "Rh": 2237, "Pd": 1828, "Ag": 1234.93, "Cd": 594.22, "Hf": 2506, "Ta": 3290, "W": 3695, "Re": 3459, "Os": 3306, "Ir": 2719, "Pt": 2041.4, "Au": 1337.33, "Hg": 234.32,
        "La": 1193, "Ce": 1068, "Pr": 1208, "Nd": 1297, "Sm": 1345, "Eu": 1095, "Gd": 1585, "Tb": 1629, "Dy": 1680, "Ho": 1747, "Er": 1802, "Tm": 1818, "Yb": 1092, "Lu": 1936,
        "Y": 1799, "Sc": 1814,
        "U": 1405.3, "Th": 2023, "Pu": 913, "Am": 1449
    }
    comp = structure.composition
    mp_list = []
    for el, amt in comp.get_el_amt_dict().items():
        mp = mp_melting_points.get(el, None)
        if mp:
            mp_list.extend([mp] * int(amt))
    if not mp_list:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.mean(mp_list))
R19 Phase Stability Window

Categorical or continuous representation of energy-above-hull stability windows.

Reasons specifically referencing low energy above the convex hull or small decomposition energy as evidence for phase stability and synthesizability.

def rule_19_phase_stability_window(structure, mp_id=None):
    try:
        if mp_id is None:
            return 0.0
        doc_data = _summary_doc(mp_id)
        eah = doc_data.get("energy_above_hull")
        if eah is not None:
            if eah < 0.1:
                return 0.0
            elif eah < 0.2:
                return 0.5
            else:
                return 1.0
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[rule_19] API fail: {e}")
    return 0.0
R20 Atomic Radii Mismatch

Standard deviation of atomic radii normalized by their mean.

Reasons discussing matching or compatible ionic/atomic radii or tolerance factors to minimize lattice strain and promote stable lattice formation.

def rule_20_atomic_radii_mismatch(structure):
    comp = structure.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
    radii = []
    for el, amt in comp.items():
        try:
            r = Element(el).atomic_radius
            if r is not None:
                radii.extend([r] * int(amt))
        except:
            continue
    if not radii or len(radii) < 2:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.std(radii) / np.mean(radii))
R21 Atoms Per Unit Cell

Number of atomic sites in the unit cell.

Reasons stating that moderate unit cell size, low structural complexity, or simple stoichiometry favors easier synthesis and reproducibility.

def rule_21_atoms_per_unit_cell(structure):
    return float(len(structure.sites))
R22 Electronegativity Difference

Difference between maximum and minimum elemental electronegativity.

Reasons discussing reasonable or moderate electronegativity differences among constituent elements to ensure stable bonding and phase formation.

def rule_22_electronegativity_difference(structure):
    comp = structure.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
    en_values = [Element(el).X for el in comp if Element(el).X is not None]
    if not en_values:
        return 0.0
    return float(max(en_values) - min(en_values))
R23 Synthesis Analogue Count

Number of known Materials Project entries in the same chemical system.

Reasons discussing the presence or absence of stable polymorphs, competing phases, or phase diagram favorability affecting synthesis.

def rule_23_synthesis_analogue_count(structure):
    chemsys = "-".join(sorted([el.symbol for el in structure.composition.elements]))
    return _chemsys_count(chemsys)
R24 Fraction Fully Occupied Sites

Fraction of crystallographic sites with full occupancy.

Reasons stating that fully occupied, ordered atomic sites, low disorder, and low defect formation energy are beneficial for phase purity and synthesis.

def rule_24_fraction_fully_occupied_sites(structure):
    total_sites = len(structure.sites)
    fully_occupied = sum(1 for site in structure.sites if all(oc == 1.0 for _, oc in site.species.items()))
    return float(fully_occupied) / total_sites if total_sites > 0 else 0.0
R25 Void Fraction

Estimated unoccupied volume fraction from atomic volumes and unit-cell volume.

Reasons highlighting low or moderate porosity, absence of excessive voids, and realistic packing for mechanical integrity and stability.

def rule_25_void_fraction(structure):
    try:
        total_volume = structure.lattice.volume
        atom_volumes = 0.0
        for site in structure:
            r = Element(site.specie.symbol).atomic_radius
            if r:
                atom_volumes += (4/3) * np.pi * r**3
        void_fraction = 1.0 - (atom_volumes / total_volume)
        return float(max(0.0, void_fraction))
    except:
        return 0.0
R26 Polyhedral Connectivity

Connectivity of coordinated framework cations or local coordination polyhedra.

Reasons mentioning well-connected polyhedra, robust network structures, or common connectivity motifs (e.g., corner/edge sharing) as aiding stability and synthesis.

def rule_26_polyhedral_connectivity(structure):
    cnn = CrystalNN()
    poly_connectivities = []
    for i, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
        try:
            el = Element(site.specie.symbol)
        except:
            continue
        if el.is_transition_metal or el.is_lanthanoid or el.is_actinoid or el.symbol in ["Al", "Si", "P", "B"]:
            try:
                neighbors = cnn.get_nn_info(structure, i)
                poly_connectivities.append(len(neighbors))
            except:
                continue
    return float(np.mean(poly_connectivities)) if poly_connectivities else 0.0
R27 Absence of Mixed Valence

Preference for a single or chemically consistent valence state.

Reasons focusing on the avoidance of rare, highly unusual, or unstable oxidation or valence states for chemical plausibility and stability.

def rule_27_absence_of_mixed_valence(structure):
    comp = structure.composition
    elements = comp.elements
    analyzer = BVAnalyzer()
    try:
        valences = analyzer.get_valences(structure)
    except:
        return 0.0
    valence_dict = {}
    for el, val in zip(structure.species, valences):
        sym = el.symbol
        if sym not in valence_dict:
            valence_dict[sym] = set()
        valence_dict[sym].add(round(val, 2))
    mixed_count = sum(1 for vset in valence_dict.values() if len(vset) > 1)
    return 1.0 - mixed_count / len(valence_dict) if valence_dict else 1.0
R28 Min Interatomic Distance

Shortest pairwise interatomic distance in the structure.

Reasons highlighting the avoidance of unphysically short or long interatomic distances, atomic overlaps, or impossible atomic arrangements for structural stability.

def rule_28_min_interatomic_distance(structure):
    try:
        dists = structure.distance_matrix
        dists[dists == 0] = np.inf
        return float(np.min(dists))
    except:
        return 0.0
R29 Absence of Unstable Motifs

Heuristic score for avoiding rare or unstable local coordination motifs.

Reasons stating that rare, unprecedented, or highly unstable structural motifs reduce the likelihood of synthesis, while common motifs favor it.

def rule_29_absence_unstable_motifs(structure):
    cn = CrystalNN()
    highly_unstable_motifs = 0
    for site in structure.sites:
        # Use the site's Element or Species object directly.
        el = site.specie
        if Element(el.symbol).is_metal and el.symbol not in [
            "Li", "Na", "K", "Rb", "Cs", "Mg", "Ca", "Sr", "Ba", "Al", "Sc", "Y", "Ti", "Zr", "Hf", "V", "Nb", "Ta",
            "Cr", "Mo", "W", "Mn", "Fe", "Co", "Ni", "Cu", "Zn", "Ga", "In", "Sn", "Pb", "Sb", "Bi"
        ]:
            highly_unstable_motifs += 1
        try:
            cn_num = cn.get_cn(structure, site)
            if el.symbol == "O" and cn_num < 2:
                highly_unstable_motifs += 1
            if el.symbol == "N" and cn_num < 2:
                highly_unstable_motifs += 1
            if el.symbol == "F" and cn_num < 1:
                highly_unstable_motifs += 1
            if el.symbol == "Cl" and cn_num < 1:
                highly_unstable_motifs += 1
        except:
            continue
    return float(highly_unstable_motifs) / len(structure.sites) if structure.sites else 0.0
R30 Elastic Anisotropy

Zener-ratio-like or lattice-anisotropy-based proxy for elastic anisotropy.

Reasons referencing that extreme mechanical properties (e.g., too brittle/soft, negative elastic constants) hinder synthesis, while moderate properties support it.

def rule_30_elastic_anisotropy(structure):
    a, b, c = structure.lattice.abc
    tol = 1e-2
    if abs(a - b) < tol and abs(b - c) < tol:
        vnn = VoronoiNN()
        bond_lengths = []
        bond_angles = []
        for i, site in enumerate(structure):
            neighs = vnn.get_nn_info(structure, i)
            for n in neighs:
                bond_lengths.append(n['weight'])
            coords = [n['site'].coords for n in neighs]
            center = site.coords
            for j in range(len(coords)):
                for k in range(j+1, len(coords)):
                    v1 = coords[j] - center
                    v2 = coords[k] - center
                    norm1 = np.linalg.norm(v1)
                    norm2 = np.linalg.norm(v2)
                    if norm1 > 1e-3 and norm2 > 1e-3:
                        cos_angle = np.dot(v1, v2) / (norm1 * norm2)
                        angle = np.arccos(np.clip(cos_angle, -1, 1))
                        bond_angles.append(np.degrees(angle))
        if bond_lengths and bond_angles:
            c11 = 1.0 / np.mean(bond_lengths)
            c44 = 1.0 / np.std(bond_angles)
            c12 = c11 * 0.5
            if c11 - c12 != 0:
                zener = 2 * c44 / (c11 - c12)
                return float(zener)
    return float(max(a, b, c) / min(a, b, c))
R31 Defect Tolerance Indicator

Variability in coordination environments as a proxy for defect tolerance.

Reasons mentioning low defect formation energy, absence of intrinsic defects, or low defect propensity as supporting phase purity and synthesis.

def rule_31_defect_tolerance_indicator(structure):
    vnn = VoronoiNN()
    site_fractions = []
    for i, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
        try:
            nns = vnn.get_nn_info(structure, i)
            site_fractions.append(len(nns))
        except:
            continue
    if not site_fractions:
        return 0.0
    mean_cn = np.mean(site_fractions)
    std_cn = np.std(site_fractions)
    return float(std_cn / mean_cn) if mean_cn != 0 else 0.0
R32 Max Coordination Number

Maximum coordination number among all atomic sites.

Reasons noting that highly strained, rare, or unusual coordination environments hinder synthesis, while typical environments support it.

def rule_32_max_coordination_number(structure):
    cnn = CrystalNN()
    try:
        coord_numbers = [cnn.get_cn(structure, i) for i in range(len(structure))]
        return float(max(coord_numbers))
    except:
        return 0.0
R33 Unstable Magnetic/Electronic States

Heuristic indicator based on partially filled d- or f-orbital configurations.

Reasons noting that unfavorable, rare, or unstable magnetic or electronic states complicate synthesis, while simple configurations favor it.

def rule_33_unstable_magnetic_electronic_states_score(structure):
    comp = structure.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
    def is_unstable_magnetic_element(el):
        e = Element(el)
        if e.is_transition_metal or e.is_lanthanoid or e.is_actinoid:
            nval = e.full_electronic_structure
            for orb in nval:
                if len(orb) == 4:
                    if orb[1] == 'd' and 0 < orb[3] < 10:
                        return True
                    if orb[1] == 'f' and 0 < orb[3] < 14:
                        return True
        return False
    unstable_count = sum(amt for el, amt in comp.items() if is_unstable_magnetic_element(el))
    total = sum(comp.values())
    return float(unstable_count) / total if total > 0 else 0.0
R34 Absence of Unstable/Volatile Elements

Score reflecting avoidance of volatile or chemically unstable elements.

Reasons stating that low polymorphic variability and lack of energetically close competing phases reduce synthesis complexity and favor phase purity.

def rule_34_absence_unstable_volatile_elements(structure):
    unstable_volatile_elements = {
        "He", "Ne", "Ar", "Kr", "Xe", "Rn", "Og",
        "Tc", "Pm", "Po", "At", "Fr", "Ra", "Ac", "Th", "Pa", "U", "Np", "Pu", "Am", "Cm", "Bk", "Cf", "Es", "Fm", "Md", "No", "Lr", "Rf", "Db", "Sg", "Bh", "Hs", "Mt", "Ds", "Rg", "Cn", "Fl", "Lv", "Ts",
        "Hg", "Cd", "Se", "Br", "I"
    }
    comp = structure.composition
    total = comp.num_atoms
    count = sum(comp[el] for el in comp.elements if el.symbol in unstable_volatile_elements)
    return 1.0 - (count / total) if total > 0 else 1.0
R35 Lattice Mismatch Minimum

Minimum mismatch between the target lattice and known lattice motifs.

Reasons highlighting that extremely large or small lattice constants indicate instability, while reasonable parameters support synthesis.

def rule_35_lattice_mismatch_min(structure):
    known_lattices = [
        (3.905, 3.905, 3.905),
        (3.868, 3.868, 3.868),
        (4.213, 4.213, 4.213),
        (5.431, 5.431, 5.431),
        (3.615, 3.615, 3.615),
        (4.758, 4.758, 12.991),
    ]
    a, b, c = sorted(structure.lattice.abc)
    min_mismatch = 1.0
    for klat in known_lattices:
        ka, kb, kc = sorted(klat)
        mismatches = [
            abs(a - ka) / ka,
            abs(b - kb) / kb,
            abs(c - kc) / kc
        ]
        mismatch = min(mismatches)
        if mismatch < min_mismatch:
            min_mismatch = mismatch
    return float(min_mismatch)
R36 Polyhedral Distortion Index

Bond-length nonuniformity within local coordination polyhedra.

Reasons noting that unusual or disconnected polyhedral networks hinder synthesis, while robust connectivity supports it.

def rule_36_polyhedral_distortion_index(structure):
    cnn = CrystalNN()
    distortions = []
    for i, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
        try:
            nns = cnn.get_nn_info(structure, i)
            if not nns:
                continue
            neighbor_coords = [nn['site'].coords for nn in nns]
            center = site.coords
            distances = [np.linalg.norm(center - nc) for nc in neighbor_coords]
            mean_dist = np.mean(distances)
            if mean_dist == 0:
                continue
            distortion = np.mean([abs(d - mean_dist) / mean_dist for d in distances])
            distortions.append(distortion)
        except:
            continue
    return float(np.mean(distortions)) if distortions else 0.0
R37 Dimensional Anisotropy

Ratio or spread of lattice parameters, such as maximum divided by minimum lattice length.

Reasons referencing known stable dimensional motifs (e.g., 2D, 3D) as supporting synthesis, and avoidance of extreme low- or high-dimensional structures.

def rule_37_dimensional_anisotropy(structure):
    lengths = np.array(structure.lattice.abc)
    max_len = np.max(lengths)
    min_len = np.min(lengths)
    return float(max_len / min_len) if min_len > 0 else 0.0
R38 Min Site Occupancy

Lowest site occupancy value in the crystal structure.

Reasons highlighting the avoidance of extremely short or long bonds or angles for stability, and the presence of typical values as supporting synthesis.

def rule_38_min_site_occupancy(structure):
    try:
        occs = [max([frac for _, frac in site.species.items()]) for site in structure.sites]
        return float(min(occs)) if occs else 0.0
    except:
        return 0.0
R39 Fraction Metallic Bonding

Fraction of near-neighbor bonds involving metal–metal pairs.

Reasons noting that highly unusual band structures (e.g., flat bands, extreme metallicity) complicate synthesis, while typical structures favor it.

def rule_39_fraction_metallic_bonding(structure):
    metallic_count = 0
    total_bonds = 0
    neighbors = structure.get_all_neighbors(r=3.0)
    for i, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
        el1 = Element(site.specie.symbol)
        for neighbor in neighbors[i]:
            # Handle both neighbor wrappers and site objects.
            try:
                el2 = Element(neighbor.site.specie.symbol)
            except AttributeError:
                el2 = Element(neighbor.specie.symbol)
            if el1.is_metal and el2.is_metal:
                metallic_count += 1
            total_bonds += 1
    return metallic_count / total_bonds if total_bonds > 0 else 0.0
R40 Interlayer Distance Std

Standard deviation of interlayer or c-axis neighbor distances.

Reasons referencing that extreme or impractical synthesis conditions (e.g., pressure, temperature, precursor availability) hinder feasibility, while standard conditions favor it.

def rule_40_interlayer_distance_std(structure):
    coords = np.array([site.frac_coords for site in structure.sites])
    c_axis = structure.lattice.matrix[2] / np.linalg.norm(structure.lattice.matrix[2])
    vnn = VoronoiNN()
    interlayer_distances = []
    for i, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
        neighbors = vnn.get_nn_info(structure, i)
        for n in neighbors:
            j = n['site_index']
            dz = np.dot(coords[j] - coords[i], c_axis)
            dz = dz - np.round(dz)
            abs_dz = abs(dz * np.linalg.norm(structure.lattice.c))
            if abs_dz > 2.0:
                interlayer_distances.append(abs_dz)
    return float(np.std(interlayer_distances)) if interlayer_distances else 0.0
R41 Framework Density

Number of atoms per unit volume.

Reasons referencing the avoidance of highly inefficient or unstable packing as important for structural integrity and synthesis.

def rule_41_framework_density(structure):
    v = structure.lattice.volume
    n_atoms = len(structure.sites)
    return n_atoms / v if v > 0 else 0.0
R42 Stoichiometry Deviation

Standard deviation or irregularity of the reduced stoichiometric coefficients.

Reasons referencing simple stoichiometry, fewer constituent elements, or chemical composition within known feasible or previously synthesized ranges.

def rule_42_stoichiometry_deviation(structure):
    comp = structure.composition
    reduced = comp.reduced_composition
    ratios = [amt / min(reduced.values()) for amt in reduced.values()]
    deviation = np.std(ratios)
    return float(deviation)
R43 Charge Balance Deviation

Per-site or composition-level deviation from charge balance.

Reasons mentioning that unrealistic or highly imbalanced charge distributions hinder stability and synthesis, while balanced distributions support it.

def rule_43_charge_balance_deviation(structure):
    try:
        valences = BVAnalyzer().get_valences(structure)
        total_charge = sum(valences)
        n_sites = len(structure.sites)
        return abs(total_charge) / n_sites if n_sites > 0 else 0.0
    except Exception:
        # Estimate net charge from the composition if bond valences are unavailable.
        oxi_table = {"O": -2, "N": -3, "F": -1, "Cl": -1, "Br": -1, "I": -1, "S": -2, "Se": -2,
                     "Li": 1, "Na": 1, "K": 1, "Mg": 2, "Ca": 2, "Al": 3, "Si": 4, "P": 5, "Fe": 3, "Cu": 2, "Zn": 2}
        comp = structure.composition
        charge = 0.0
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            charge += oxi_table.get(el.symbol, 0) * amt
        n_sites = len(structure.sites)
        return abs(float(charge)) / n_sites if n_sites > 0 else 0.0
R44 Site Disorder Score

Occupancy-based measure of crystallographic site disorder.

Reasons noting that unusual, complex, or partial site occupancy patterns complicate synthesis, while full occupancy supports it.

def rule_44_site_disorder_score(structure):
    disorder_scores = []
    for site in structure.sites:
        occs = list(site.species.values())
        if len(occs) == 1 and occs[0] == 1.0:
            disorder_scores.append(0.0)
        else:
            max_occ = max(occs)
            disorder_scores.append(1.0 - max_occ)
    return float(np.mean(disorder_scores)) if disorder_scores else 0.0
R45 Min Antisite Formation-Energy Proxy

Proxy based on cation electronegativity similarity, related to antisite disorder tendency.

Reasons referencing a low minimum electronegativity difference between cations in a compound, which is associated with a reduced tendency for antisite defect formation. When two or more cations have similar electronegativities, it becomes energetically less favorable for them to swap lattice sites (antisite disorder), thereby supporting the formation of a more ordered and synthesizable structure.

def rule_45_min_antisite_formation_energy(structure):
    elements = list(structure.composition.elements)
    cations = [el for el in elements if Element(el).is_metal]
    if len(cations) < 2:
        return 0.0
    en_diffs = []
    for i, el1 in enumerate(cations):
        for el2 in cations[i+1:]:
            en1 = Element(el1).X
            en2 = Element(el2).X
            en_diffs.append(abs(en1 - en2))
    if not en_diffs:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.min(en_diffs))
R46 Surface Energy Proxy

Fraction of undercoordinated sites as a proxy for surface or interface instability.

Reasons referencing the need for reasonable surface energy, stable surface terminations, or interface compatibility to facilitate nucleation and growth.

def rule_46_surface_energy_proxy(structure):
    vnn = VoronoiNN()
    undercoord_fractions = []
    for site in structure.sites:
        try:
            ideal_cn = site.specie.max_coordinated_neighbors
        except AttributeError:
            ideal_cn = None
        if ideal_cn is None:
            Z = site.specie.Z
            if Z <= 2:
                ideal_cn = 2
            elif Z <= 10:
                ideal_cn = 4
            elif Z <= 18:
                ideal_cn = 4
            elif Z <= 54:
                ideal_cn = 6
            else:
                ideal_cn = 6
        try:
            actual_cn = len(vnn.get_nn_info(structure, site.index))
        except:
            actual_cn = 0
        undercoord = max(0, ideal_cn - actual_cn) / ideal_cn
        undercoord_fractions.append(undercoord)
    return float(np.mean(undercoord_fractions)) if undercoord_fractions else 0.0
R47 Synthesis Complexity Proxy

Composite score based on number of elements, rare-earth content, and unusual coordination.

Reasons referencing the lack of need for rare, unstable, or inaccessible precursors, and compatibility with known synthesis routes as supporting feasibility.

def rule_47_synthesis_complexity_proxy(structure):
    elements = [el.symbol for el in structure.composition.elements]
    n_elements = len(elements)
    rare_earths = set([
        "Sc", "Y", "La", "Ce", "Pr", "Nd", "Pm", "Sm", "Eu", "Gd", "Tb", "Dy", "Ho", "Er", "Tm", "Yb", "Lu"
    ])
    n_rare_earths = sum(1 for el in elements if el in rare_earths)
    n_metalloids = sum(1 for el in elements if Element(el).is_metalloid)
    try:
        vnn = VoronoiNN()
        n_unusual_coord = 0
        for site in structure:
            try:
                cn = vnn.get_cn(structure, site)
                if cn < 2 or cn > 8:
                    n_unusual_coord += 1
            except:
                continue
        frac_unusual_coord = n_unusual_coord / len(structure)
    except:
        frac_unusual_coord = 0.0
    return float(0.5 * n_elements + n_rare_earths + n_metalloids + 5 * frac_unusual_coord)
R48 Max Thermodynamic Stability Deviation

Deviation of formation energy per atom or related thermodynamic quantity.

Reasons referencing the avoidance of highly unstable or negative thermal properties (e.g., expansion, decomposition) as aiding synthesis.

def rule_48_max_thermodynamic_stability_deviation(structure, mp_id=None, api_key=API_KEY):
    e_per_atom = None
    if mp_id is not None:
        doc_data = _summary_doc(mp_id)
        if doc_data and doc_data.get("formation_energy_per_atom") is not None:
            e_per_atom = float(doc_data["formation_energy_per_atom"])
        else:
            print(f"[WARN] 'formation_energy_per_atom' not found or is None for {mp_id}")
    else:
        comp = structure.composition.reduced_formula
        with MPRester(api_key) as mpr:
            docs = mpr.materials.summary.search(
                formula=comp,
                fields=["material_id", "formation_energy_per_atom"]
            )
        if docs:
            doc_data = docs[0].model_dump() if hasattr(docs[0], "model_dump") else docs[0].dict()
            if "formation_energy_per_atom" in doc_data and doc_data["formation_energy_per_atom"] is not None:
                e_per_atom = float(doc_data["formation_energy_per_atom"])
            else:
                print(f"[WARN] 'formation_energy_per_atom' not found or is None for {comp}")
        else:
            print(f"[WARN] No summary found for {comp}")
    if e_per_atom is None:
        return 0.0

    lower, upper = -6.0, 1.0
    if e_per_atom < lower:
        return abs(e_per_atom - lower)
    elif e_per_atom > upper:
        return abs(e_per_atom - upper)
    else:
        return 1.0 / (1.0 + abs(e_per_atom))
R49 Min Migration Barrier Proxy

Proxy based on mobile-ion radius and structural density.

Reasons referencing low atomic displacement parameters (ADPs), moderate atomic vibrations, and the absence of extreme atomic displacements as supporting structural reliability.

def rule_49_min_migration_barrier(structure):
    from pymatgen.core.periodic_table import Element
    try:
        mobile_ions = [el for el in structure.composition.elements if Element(el).is_alkali or Element(el).is_alkaline]
        if not mobile_ions:
            return 0.0
        radii = [Element(el).ionic_radii.get(1, Element(el).atomic_radius) for el in mobile_ions]
        min_radius = min([r for r in radii if r is not None])
        density = structure.density if structure.density > 0 else 1.0
        return float(min_radius / density)
    except:
        return 0.0
R50 Environmental Sensitivity Proxy

Score reflecting the presence of air-, moisture-, or chemically reactive elements.

Reasons referencing low configurational entropy, minimal disorder, and the absence of excessive entropy as favoring stable phase formation and synthesis.

def rule_50_env_sensitivity_proxy(structure):
    reactive_elements = {"Li", "Na", "K", "Rb", "Cs", "F", "Cl", "Br", "I", "O", "S", "Se", "Te"}
    comp = structure.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
    total = sum(comp.values())
    if total == 0:
        return 0.0
    reactive_amt = sum(amt for el, amt in comp.items() if el in reactive_elements)
    return 1.0 - (reactive_amt / total)

Original · Formation energy 25 rules

R01 Atomic packing fraction

Sum of the atomic-sphere volumes calculated from tabulated atomic radii, divided by unit-cell volume.

Crystals with a high global unit-cell atomic packing fraction—sum of element hard‑sphere volumes (from fixed tabulated radii) divided by the CIF unit-cell volume—tend to have lower formation energy per atom by minimizing voids and undercoordination, thus stabilizing bonding networks.

def rule_01_crystals_a_high_global_unit(structure):
    try:
        total_hard_sphere_volume = 0.0
        for site in structure.sites:
            symbol = site.specie.symbol
            try:
                radius = Element(symbol).atomic_radius
            except Exception:
                radius = None
            if radius is None:
                return 0.0
            atom_volume = (4.0/3.0) * np.pi * (radius ** 3)
            total_hard_sphere_volume += atom_volume
        cell_volume = structure.volume
        if cell_volume <= 0:
            return 0.0
        packing_fraction = total_hard_sphere_volume / cell_volume
        if not (np.isfinite(packing_fraction) and packing_fraction > 0):
            return 0.0
        return float(packing_fraction)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R02 Charge-neutral oxidation-state magnitude

Largest stoichiometry-weighted mean absolute oxidation state among charge-neutral assignments of common elemental valences.

Assign common oxidation states to each element so the formula is charge neutral (use composition and, if available, CIF-guided coordination/bond-valence cues). Compute the composition-weighted mean absolute formal oxidation state. Compounds that admit a neutral assignment with larger mean |z| are typically more stabilized by ionic (Madelung) interactions; if neutrality cannot be satisfied with common states, the material is prone to higher energy due to charge imbalance/redox frustration.

def rule_02_assign_common_oxidation_states_to(structure):
    common_oxidation_state_ranges = {
        'H': [-1, 1], 'He': [0], 'Li': [1], 'Be': [2], 'B': [3], 'C': [-4, 4], 'N': [-3, 3, 5], 'O': [-2],
        'F': [-1], 'Ne': [0], 'Na': [1], 'Mg': [2], 'Al': [3], 'Si': [-4, 4], 'P': [-3, 3, 5], 'S': [-2, 4, 6],
        'Cl': [-1, 1, 5, 7], 'Ar': [0], 'K': [1], 'Ca': [2], 'Sc': [3], 'Ti': [3, 4], 'V': [3, 4, 5],
        'Cr': [2, 3, 6], 'Mn': [2, 4, 7], 'Fe': [2, 3], 'Co': [2, 3], 'Ni': [2, 3], 'Cu': [1, 2],
        'Zn': [2], 'Ga': [3], 'Ge': [2, 4], 'As': [-3, 3, 5], 'Se': [-2, 4, 6], 'Br': [-1, 1, 5],
        'Kr': [0, 2], 'Rb': [1], 'Sr': [2], 'Y': [3], 'Zr': [4], 'Nb': [5], 'Mo': [4, 6], 'Tc': [7],
        'Ru': [3, 4], 'Rh': [3], 'Pd': [2, 4], 'Ag': [1], 'Cd': [2], 'In': [3], 'Sn': [2, 4],
        'Sb': [-3, 3, 5], 'Te': [-2, 4, 6], 'I': [-1, 1, 5, 7], 'Xe': [0, 2, 4, 6, 8], 'Cs': [1],
        'Ba': [2], 'La': [3], 'Ce': [3, 4], 'Pr': [3, 4], 'Nd': [3], 'Pm': [3], 'Sm': [2, 3],
        'Eu': [2, 3], 'Gd': [3], 'Tb': [3, 4], 'Dy': [3], 'Ho': [3], 'Er': [3], 'Tm': [2, 3],
        'Yb': [2, 3], 'Lu': [3], 'Hf': [4], 'Ta': [5], 'W': [4, 6], 'Re': [4, 6, 7],
        'Os': [4, 6], 'Ir': [3, 4], 'Pt': [2, 4], 'Au': [1, 3], 'Hg': [1, 2], 'Tl': [1, 3],
        'Pb': [2, 4], 'Bi': [3, 5], 'Po': [-2, 2, 4, 6], 'At': [-1, 1, 3, 5, 7], 'Rn': [0, 2, 4],
        'Fr': [1], 'Ra': [2], 'Ac': [3], 'Th': [4], 'Pa': [5], 'U': [4, 6], 'Np': [3, 4, 5, 6, 7],
        'Pu': [3, 4, 5, 6], 'Am': [3, 4, 5, 6], 'Cm': [3]
    }
    try:
        formula_elements = list(structure.composition.elements)
        element_symbols = [el.symbol for el in formula_elements]
        stoichs = [structure.composition.get_atomic_fraction(el) * structure.composition.num_atoms for el in formula_elements]
        stoichs = [float(round(s)) for s in stoichs]
        oxidation_state_lists = []
        for el in formula_elements:
            try:
                states = common_oxidation_state_ranges.get(el.symbol, list(el.common_oxidation_states))
                if len(states) == 0:
                    states = [0]
            except Exception:
                states = [0]
            oxidation_state_lists.append(states)
        min_abs_mean = None
        best_assign = None
        for state_tuple in itertools.product(*oxidation_state_lists):
            total_charge = sum(z * n for z, n in zip(state_tuple, stoichs))
            if abs(total_charge) < 1e-3:
                abs_mean = sum(abs(z) * n for z, n in zip(state_tuple, stoichs)) / sum(stoichs)
                if (min_abs_mean is None) or (abs_mean > min_abs_mean):
                    min_abs_mean = abs_mean
                    best_assign = state_tuple
        if best_assign is not None and np.isfinite(min_abs_mean):
            return float(min_abs_mean)
        else:
            return 0.0
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R03 Relative radius dispersion

Composition-weighted standard deviation of elemental radii divided by their weighted mean; ionic radii are used when available.

Minimize composition-weighted dispersion of constituent radii: a high coefficient of variation of atomic/ionic radii (std/mean across elements, occupancy-weighted) signals elastic misfit; structures with lower radius polydispersity tend to have lower formation energy per atom.

def rule_03_minimize_composition_weighted_dispersion_of(structure):
    try:
        composition = structure.composition
        element_types = list(composition.elements)
        occupancies = np.array([composition.get_atomic_fraction(el) for el in element_types])
        radii = []
        for el in element_types:
            r = None
            try:
                r = Element(el.symbol).average_ionic_radius
            except Exception:
                r = None
            if r is None:
                r = Element(el.symbol).atomic_radius
            if r is None or np.isnan(r):
                r = 0.0
            radii.append(r)
        radii = np.array(radii, dtype=float)
        if np.any(radii <= 0):
            return 0.0
        weighted_mean = np.sum(radii * occupancies)
        weighted_squared = np.sum((radii ** 2) * occupancies)
        weighted_var = weighted_squared - weighted_mean ** 2
        weighted_std = np.sqrt(max(weighted_var, 0.0))
        if weighted_mean <= 0:
            return 0.0
        return float(weighted_std / weighted_mean)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R04 Metal fraction

Fraction of atoms assigned to metallic elements.

Use the atomic fraction of metallic elements in the composition (f_metal) from the CIF stoichiometry. Intermediate f_metal (~0.3–0.8) indicates substantial metal–nonmetal pairing, favoring heteropolar ionic/covalent bonds and typically more exothermic formation energies; very high or very low f_metal implies predominantly metallic or homopolar networks with a weaker compound-forming drive.

def rule_04_use_atomic_fraction_of_metallic(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition
        total_atoms = comp.num_atoms
        if total_atoms == 0:
            return 0.0
        metallic_count = 0.0
        for el, amt in comp.get_el_amt_dict().items():
            try:
                element = Element(el)
                if hasattr(element, 'is_metal') and element.is_metal:
                    metallic_count += amt
            except Exception:
                continue
        return float(metallic_count / total_atoms)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R05 First ionization energy

Atomic-fraction-weighted mean of the tabulated first ionization energies.

Use the composition-weighted mean first ionization energy of the constituent elements to gauge charge-transfer capacity: lower averages enable easier cation formation and more ionic bonding; higher averages resist electron removal and bias toward more covalent bonding.

def rule_05_use_composition_weighted_mean_first(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition
        total_atoms = comp.num_atoms
        if total_atoms <= 0:
            return 0.0
        elem_fracs = comp.fractional_composition.items()
        s = 0.0
        for el, frac in elem_fracs:
            E = Element(str(el))
            ionization_energies = getattr(E, 'ionization_energies', None)
            if ionization_energies and len(ionization_energies) > 0 and ionization_energies[0] is not None:
                s += frac * float(ionization_energies[0])
            else:
                return 0.0
        return float(s)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R06 Coordination-number variance

Multiplicity-weighted variance of Voronoi neighbor counts across symmetry-distinct sites.

Use the multiplicity-weighted variance of per-site coordination numbers (CN) computed from a CIF (neighbors via Voronoi or element-radius cutoffs). Structures with low CN variance—i.e., uniform coordination—tend to be lower in formation energy, whereas high variance, especially with many undercoordinated sites, signals unsaturated/strained bonds and higher energy.

def rule_06_use_multiplicity_weighted_variance_of(structure):
    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(structure)
        symm_struct = sga.get_symmetrized_structure()
        coord_numbers = []
        multiplicities = []
        vnn = VoronoiNN()
        for eq_sites in symm_struct.equivalent_sites:
            site = eq_sites[0]
            try:
                n = len(vnn.get_nn_info(structure, structure.sites.index(site)))
            except Exception:
                n = 0
            coord_numbers.append(float(n))
            multiplicities.append(len(eq_sites))
        if len(coord_numbers) == 0 or any(np.isnan(cn) for cn in coord_numbers):
            return 0.0
        multiplicities = np.array(multiplicities, dtype=float)
        coord_numbers = np.array(coord_numbers, dtype=float)
        weights = multiplicities / multiplicities.sum()
        avg = np.dot(coord_numbers, weights)
        var = np.dot(weights, (coord_numbers - avg) ** 2)
        if not np.isfinite(var):
            return 0.0
        return float(var)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R07 Mean site radius

Occupancy-weighted mean of available site-species radii, using ionic radii or a covalent-radius fallback.

Use the site-occupancy-weighted mean atomic radius as the primary size scale: compositions whose mean radius allows dense, strain-minimized coordination in the given structure tend to be more stable. Compute r_mean = Σ(occ_i·r_i)/Σ(occ_i) from CIF species and site occupancies using fixed elemental covalent radii; where oxidation/coordination are clear, substitute Shannon ionic radii for those sites.

def rule_07_use_site_occupancy_weighted_mean(structure):
    try:
        total_weight = 0.0
        weighted_sum = 0.0
        cnn = CrystalNN()
        for site_idx, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
            for sp, occ in site.species.items():
                r = None
                oxi = getattr(sp, 'oxi_state', None)
                if oxi is not None:
                    try:
                        cn = cnn.get_cn(structure, site_idx)
                        try:
                            str_cn = {4: 'IV', 5: 'V', 6: 'VI', 8: 'VIII'}.get(int(round(cn)), 'VI')
                        except Exception:
                            str_cn = 'VI'
                        r = getattr(sp, 'ionic_radii', {}).get((oxi, str_cn), None)
                        if r is None:
                            r = getattr(sp, 'ionic_radius', None)
                    except Exception:
                        r = getattr(sp, 'ionic_radius', None)
                if r is None or (hasattr(r, '__le__') and r <= 0):
                    try:
                        r = getattr(sp, 'covalent_radius', None)
                    except Exception:
                        r = None
                if r is None or (hasattr(r, '__le__') and r <= 0):
                    continue
                weighted_sum += occ * r
                total_weight += occ
        if total_weight > 0.0 and np.isfinite(weighted_sum / total_weight):
            return float(weighted_sum / total_weight)
        else:
            return 0.0
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R08 Local electronegativity contrast

Sum of Voronoi-weighted absolute electronegativity differences between neighboring sites, divided by the number of sites.

High coordination-weighted mean electronegativity difference (Pauling) across nearest-neighbor bonds favors ionic character and typically yields more exothermic formation energies. From a CIF, construct the bond network (e.g., Voronoi or distance cutoff), compute |χi−χj| for each bond, weight by bond multiplicity per atom, and average per atom.

def rule_08_high_coordination_weighted_mean_electronegativity(structure):
    try:
        vnn = VoronoiNN()
        n_sites = len(structure.sites)
        bond_diffs = []
        for idx, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
            try:
                neighbors = vnn.get_nn_info(structure, idx)
            except Exception:
                continue
            site_elem = site.specie.element if hasattr(site.specie, "element") else Element(str(site.specie))
            xi = site_elem.X
            site_weights = []
            for nn in neighbors:
                w = nn.get('weight', 1.0)
                nn_elem = nn['site'].specie.element if hasattr(nn['site'].specie, "element") else Element(str(nn['site'].specie))
                xj = nn_elem.X
                diff = abs(xi - xj)
                site_weights.append(w * diff)
            if site_weights:
                bond_diffs.append(np.sum(site_weights))
        if bond_diffs and n_sites > 0:
            mean = float(np.sum(bond_diffs) / float(n_sites))
            if np.isfinite(mean):
                return mean
        return 0.0
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R09 Mean coordination number

Multiplicity-weighted mean of site coordination numbers obtained from Voronoi neighbors.

Multiplicity-weighted mean coordination number (CN_avg) of all atomic sites: higher CN_avg generally stabilizes a crystal via greater bond saturation. Obtain CN_avg from a CIF by constructing a chemically sensible neighbor graph (e.g., Voronoi or distance cutoff using elemental radii), counting neighbors per site, and averaging by site multiplicity.

def rule_09_multiplicity_weighted_mean_coordination_number(structure):
    # Use available VoronoiNN and SpacegroupAnalyzer from module level names
    site_cn = []
    multiplicities = []
    vnn = VoronoiNN(allow_pathological=True)
    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(structure)
        equiv_atoms = sga.get_symmetry_dataset()["equivalent_atoms"]
    except Exception:
        equiv_atoms = [i for i in range(len(structure.sites))]
    for i, site in enumerate(structure.sites):
        try:
            cn = vnn.get_cn(structure, i)
        except Exception:
            cn = 0.0
        site_cn.append(cn)
        # Try getting the multiplicity
        if hasattr(site, 'properties') and site.properties is not None and 'multiplicity' in site.properties:
            multiplicity = site.properties['multiplicity']
        else:
            mult = sum(1 for eqi in equiv_atoms if eqi == i)
            multiplicity = mult if mult > 0 else 1
        multiplicities.append(multiplicity)
    weighted_sum = float(np.dot(site_cn, multiplicities))
    total_mult = float(np.sum(multiplicities))
    if total_mult > 0.0 and np.isfinite(weighted_sum / total_mult):
        return weighted_sum / total_mult
    return 0.0
R10 Relative bond-length mismatch

Mean absolute difference between a neighbor distance and the sum of the two atomic or ionic radii, divided by that radius sum.

Structures with a low mean absolute normalized bond-length misfit (|d_ij − (r_i+r_j)|/(r_i+r_j), averaged over first-shell neighbor pairs) tend to have lower formation energy per atom. Obtain neighbors from the CIF, take expected distances from tabulated radii (Shannon ionic if oxidation states assigned, else covalent), and average this fractional deviation; larger values indicate local bond strain and destabilization.

def rule_10_structures_a_low_mean_absolute(structure):
    struct = structure.copy()
    # Try to assign oxidation states using BVAnalyzer as fallback if add_oxidation_state_by_guess fails
    try:
        struct.add_oxidation_state_by_guess()
        oxi_state_assigned = True
    except Exception:
        oxi_state_assigned = False
        try:
            struct = BVAnalyzer().get_oxi_state_decorated_structure(struct)
            oxi_state_assigned = True
        except Exception:
            oxi_state_assigned = False
    # Try CrystalNN, fallback to VoronoiNN for neighbor list
    for nn_class in [CrystalNN, VoronoiNN]:
        try:
            nn_finder = nn_class()
            nlist = [nn_finder.get_nn_info(struct, i) for i in range(len(struct))]
            break
        except Exception:
            nlist = None
            continue
    if nlist is None:
        return 0.0
    bond_fracs = []
    for i, site in enumerate(struct):
        site_elem = site.specie.symbol
        if oxi_state_assigned and hasattr(site.specie, 'oxi_state'):
            # Try Shannon radius with oxi state
            elem_i = Element(site_elem)
            oxi_i = int(round(float(getattr(site.specie, 'oxi_state', 0))))
            r_i = elem_i.ionic_radii.get(oxi_i, elem_i.average_ionic_radius)
        else:
            r_i = Element(site_elem).atomic_radius
        if r_i is None:
            r_i = Element(site_elem).atomic_radius
        for ninfo in nlist[i]:
            neigh_spec = ninfo['site'].specie
            neigh_elem = neigh_spec.symbol
            if oxi_state_assigned and hasattr(neigh_spec, 'oxi_state'):
                elem_j = Element(neigh_elem)
                oxi_j = int(round(float(getattr(neigh_spec, 'oxi_state', 0))))
                r_j = elem_j.ionic_radii.get(oxi_j, elem_j.average_ionic_radius)
            else:
                r_j = Element(neigh_elem).atomic_radius
            if r_j is None:
                r_j = Element(neigh_elem).atomic_radius
            if r_i is None or r_j is None:
                continue
            d_ij = site.distance(ninfo['site'])
            r_sum = r_i + r_j
            if r_sum <= 0:
                continue
            frac = abs(d_ij - r_sum) / r_sum
            bond_fracs.append(frac)
    bond_fracs = [f for f in bond_fracs if np.isfinite(f)]
    if not bond_fracs:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.mean(bond_fracs))
R11 Mean valence-electron count

Composition-weighted mean of elemental valence-electron counts estimated from electronic configurations or periodic groups.

Composition-weighted mean valence electrons per atom (VEC) controls band filling; phases are favored when VEC fills bonding states but avoids significant antibonding occupation, setting the bonding capacity and metallic/covalent character for the composition.

def rule_11_composition_weighted_mean_valence_electrons(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition
        total_atoms = comp.num_atoms
        if total_atoms == 0:
            return 0.0
        total_valence_e = 0.0
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            try:
                elem = Element(el)
                val_e = elem.full_electronic_structure
                # Find the sum of electrons in the highest principal quantum number shell (valence shell)
                max_n = max([t[0] for t in val_e])
                val_electrons = sum(t[2] for t in val_e if t[0] == max_n)
                total_valence_e += val_electrons * float(amt)
            except Exception:
                group = None
                try:
                    group = Element(el).group
                except Exception:
                    return 0.0
                if group is None:
                    return 0.0
                if group <= 2:
                    val_electrons = group
                elif group <= 12:
                    val_electrons = (group - 2) + 2
                elif group <= 18:
                    val_electrons = group - 10
                else:
                    val_electrons = 0
                total_valence_e += val_electrons * float(amt)
        mean_valence = total_valence_e / total_atoms
        if not (np.isfinite(mean_valence) and mean_valence >= 0.0):
            return 0.0
        return float(mean_valence)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R12 Special-position fraction

Fraction of sites flagged from their Wyckoff multiplicity or site-symmetry symbol.

Materials with a higher fraction of atoms occupying special Wyckoff positions (site symmetry > 1 or multiplicity lower than the general position) tend to have lower formation energy per atom. Quantify by symmetrizing the CIF, assigning Wyckoff letters/site symmetries, and computing f_special = (atoms on special positions)/(total atoms).

def rule_12_materials_a_higher_fraction_of(structure):
    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(structure, symprec=0.01)
        symm_struct = sga.get_symmetrized_structure()
        dataset = sga.get_symmetry_dataset()
        wyckoff_info = dataset['wyckoffs'] if 'wyckoffs' in dataset else []
        equivalent_atoms = dataset['equivalent_atoms'] if 'equivalent_atoms' in dataset else []
        site_symmetry_symbols = dataset['site_symmetry_symbols'] if 'site_symmetry_symbols' in dataset else []
        site_multiplicities = [getattr(site, 'wyckoff_multiplicity', 1) for site in getattr(symm_struct, 'sites', [])]
        site_letters = getattr(symm_struct, 'wyckoff_letters', [None]*len(site_multiplicities))
        # Fallback general multiplicity determination
        if site_multiplicities:
            try:
                general_mult = max(site_multiplicities)
            except Exception:
                general_mult = 1
        else:
            general_mult = 1
        special_flags = []
        for idx, (site, letter, mult) in enumerate(zip(getattr(symm_struct, 'sites', []), site_letters, site_multiplicities)):
            # If multiplicity lower than general position
            if mult < general_mult:
                special_flags.append(1)
            else:
                # Check if site symmetry > 1
                sym = site_symmetry_symbols[idx] if idx < len(site_symmetry_symbols) else ''
                order = 1
                for c in str(sym):
                    if c.isdigit():
                        order = max(order, int(c))
                if order > 1:
                    special_flags.append(1)
                else:
                    special_flags.append(0)
        total = float(len(special_flags))
        if total > 0:
            f_special = float(sum(special_flags)) / total
            if np.isfinite(f_special):
                return f_special
            else:
                return 0.0
        else:
            return 0.0
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R13 Electronegativity dispersion

Composition-weighted standard deviation of elemental Pauling electronegativities.

At fixed composition, a larger composition-weighted standard deviation of elemental Pauling electronegativities (sigma_chi) promotes heteropolar charge transfer and Madelung stabilization; for similar mean electronegativity, higher sigma_chi more often yields stable ionic or mixed ionic–covalent networks.

def rule_13_at_fixed_composition_a_larger(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition
        total = comp.num_atoms
        if total == 0:
            return 0.0
        chi_list = []
        weights = []
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            try:
                ele = Element(el)
                chi = ele.X
                if chi is None:
                    return 0.0
            except Exception:
                return 0.0
            chi_list.append(chi)
            weights.append(amt)
        weights = np.array(weights)
        chi_array = np.array(chi_list)
        if weights.sum() == 0:
            return 0.0
        mean = np.average(chi_array, weights=weights)
        variance = np.average((chi_array - mean) ** 2, weights=weights)
        stddev = math.sqrt(variance)
        if not np.isfinite(stddev):
            return 0.0
        return float(stddev)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R14 Mean electronegativity

Composition-weighted mean of elemental Pauling electronegativities.

Compounds with a composition-weighted mean Pauling electronegativity in a moderate range (about 1.6–2.2) tend to achieve lower formation energies by balancing ionic and covalent bonding; too low favors metallic bonding, too high overpolarizes bonds.

def rule_14_compounds_a_composition_weighted_mean(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition
        total = comp.num_atoms
        if total == 0:
            return 0.0
        weighted_sum = 0.0
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            try:
                en = Element(str(el)).X
            except Exception:
                return 0.0
            if en is None or not np.isfinite(en):
                return 0.0
            weighted_sum += amt * en
        mean_en = weighted_sum / total
        if not np.isfinite(mean_en):
            return 0.0
        return float(mean_en)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R15 Mean atomic number

Atomic-fraction-weighted mean atomic number.

Use the composition-weighted mean atomic number, Z̄ = Σ_i x_i Z_i (x_i from CIF stoichiometry/site occupancies), as a proxy for average nuclear charge. Larger or smaller Z̄ shifts screening and valence orbital energies, altering typical bond strengths and cohesion, which in turn trends with formation energy per atom.

def rule_15_use_composition_weighted_mean_atomic(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition
        total = comp.num_atoms
        if total <= 0:
            return 0.0
        mean_Z = 0.0
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            try:
                Z = Element(str(el)).Z
                mean_Z += (amt / total) * Z
            except Exception:
                return 0.0
        if not (np.isfinite(mean_Z) and mean_Z > 0.0):
            return 0.0
        return float(mean_Z)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R16 Pairwise electronegativity contrast

Mean absolute electronegativity difference over pairs weighted by elemental atomic fractions; both pair orders are counted.

Use the composition-weighted mean pairwise electronegativity gap among constituent elements: Δχ_pair = 2 * Σ_{i<j} x_i x_j |χ_i − χ_j|, where x_i are atomic fractions from CIF site multiplicities/occupancies and χ_i are tabulated Pauling electronegativities. Larger Δχ_pair indicates stronger average charge transfer/ionic character and typically more exothermic formation energies.

def rule_16_use_composition_weighted_mean_pairwise(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition.fractional_composition
        el_amt = comp.get_el_amt_dict()
        elements = sorted(el_amt.keys())
        fractions = [comp[el] for el in elements]
        chis = []
        for el in elements:
            try:
                chis.append(Element(el).X)
            except Exception:
                return 0.0
        n = len(elements)
        if n < 2:
            return 0.0
        delta_chi = 0.0
        for i in range(n - 1):
            for j in range(i + 1, n):
                xixj = fractions[i] * fractions[j]
                dchi = abs(chis[i] - chis[j])
                delta_chi += xixj * dchi
        val = 2 * delta_chi
        return float(val) if math.isfinite(val) else 0.0
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R17 Site-mixing entropy

Multiplicity-weighted mean mixing entropy of elemental occupancies on symmetry-equivalent sites, using natural logarithms.

Compositional configurational entropy stabilizes multicomponent crystals: higher ideal-mixing entropy per atom S_conf/k_B = -∑ x_i ln x_i (from elemental fractions in the CIF formula) generally lowers formation energy. If CIF site occupancies indicate mixing on symmetry-distinct sublattices, compute sublattice entropies and weight by site multiplicities to refine the estimate; excessive partitioning over many unique sites can introduce enthalpic penalties that offset the entropic gain.

def rule_17_compositional_configurational_entropy_stabilizes_multicomponent(structure):
    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(structure, symprec=0.01)
        sym_struct = sga.get_symmetrized_structure()
        total_sites = len(structure.sites)
        # Fallback: check if equivalent_sites exists and is non-empty
        if not hasattr(sym_struct, 'equivalent_sites') or not sym_struct.equivalent_sites:
            comp = structure.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
            total = sum(comp.values())
            x_i = [amt / total for amt in comp.values() if amt > 0]
            entropy = -sum(x * np.log(x) for x in x_i) if all(x_i) else 0.0
            return float(entropy)
        total_entropy = 0.0
        total_mult = 0
        for sites in sym_struct.equivalent_sites:
            occ_dict = {}
            for site in sites:
                for sp, occ in site.species.items():
                    el = str(sp)
                    occ_dict[el] = occ_dict.get(el, 0.0) + occ
            num_sites = len(sites)
            fracs = [occ/num_sites for occ in occ_dict.values() if occ > 0]
            if len(fracs) > 1:
                s = -sum(x * np.log(x) for x in fracs)
            else:
                s = 0.0
            total_entropy += num_sites * s
            total_mult += num_sites
        if total_mult == 0:
            return 0.0
        return float(total_entropy / total_mult)
    except Exception:
        return 0.0
R18 Mean elemental cohesive energy

Composition-weighted mean of tabulated elemental cohesive energies; no crystalline compound energy is used.

Use the composition-weighted mean of the pure-element cohesive energies as a baseline bonding-energy scale: compounds whose constituents have larger mean elemental cohesive energy tend to form intrinsically stronger bonding networks and greater thermodynamic stability, all else equal.

def rule_18_use_composition_weighted_mean_of(structure):
    # Cohesive energies (in eV/atom) from Kittel or literature for main elements
    cohesive_energies = {

    try:

        if total == 0:
            return 0.0
        mean = 0.0
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            ce = cohesive_energies.get(el.symbol, None)
            if ce is None:
                return 0.0
            mean += ce * (amt / total)
        if not np.isfinite(mean):
