R01 Mean distance from a signed site bond-valence sum to an allowed common valence

Bond-valence sums (BVS) computed from observed bond lengths should closely match the formal oxidation state of each atom (within ±0.3 v.u.). Significant BVS deviations reveal charge imbalances or unrealistic bond-length distributions, indicating the structure is electronically inconsistent and therefore unlikely to be synthesized as a stable compound.

def rule_01(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def nearest_states(s,b):
        mono=len(s.composition.elements)==1
        ref=[]
        for site,obs in zip(s,b):
            z=0.
            for el,occ in site.species.items():
                q=min(vals(el.symbol,mono),key=lambda x:(abs(obs-x),abs(x),x))
                z+=occ*q
            ref.append(z/sum(site.species.values()))
        return np.asarray(ref,dtype=float)

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    def nominal_assignment(s):

        am=s.composition.element_composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        sy=sorted(am)
        if len(sy)==1: return {sy[0]:0}
        xs={k:electr(k) for k in sy}; mean=sum(am[k]*xs[k] for k in sy)/sum(am.values())
        states={0.:(0.,0.,0.,())}
        for k in sy:
            nxt={}
            common=vals(k,False)
            available=tuple(sorted(set(Element(k).oxidation_states)|set(common)))
            for net,(p,u,a,qs) in states.items():
                for q in available:
                    key=round(net+am[k]*q,8)
                    score=(p+am[k]*max(0.,q*(xs[k]-mean)),u+(0. if q in common else am[k]),a+am[k]*abs(q),qs+(q,))
                    if key not in nxt or score<nxt[key]: nxt[key]=score
            states=nxt
        net=min(states,key=lambda k:(abs(k),states[k]))
        return dict(zip(sy,states[net][3]))

    def topological_valence(s):

        assignment=nominal_assignment(s)
        occ=np.asarray([site.species.num_atoms for site in s])
        q=np.asarray([sum(v*assignment[e.symbol] for e,v in site.species.items())/o for site,o in zip(s,occ)])
        incoming=np.zeros(len(s))
        if not (np.any(q>0) and np.any(q<0)): return incoming
        distances=s.distance_matrix
        minima=np.array([np.min(distances[i,np.where(q*qi<0)[0]]) if qi!=0 else 0. for i,qi in enumerate(q)])
        cutoff=1.20*float(max(minima))+1e-6
        ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
        rows=[[] for site in s]
        for i,j,d in zip(ii,jj,dd):
            if q[i]*q[j]<0 and 1e-8<d<=1.20*minima[i]+1e-7: rows[int(i)].append(int(j))
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if not row:
                if q[i]!=0: raise ValueError("Nominal ionic site has no opposite neighbor")
                continue
            den=sum(occ[j] for j in row)
            for j in row: incoming[j]-=q[i]*occ[i]/den
        return incoming

    def bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        if any(el.symbol not in params for el in st.composition.element_composition.elements):
            return topological_valence(st)
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    b=np.asarray(bvs(s))
    return np.mean(np.abs(b-nearest_states(s,b)))
R02 Fraction of sites with extreme first-shell coordination (CN <= 2 or > 10); all-site geometry proxy, not an oxidation-specific radius test

Coordination numbers of each cation, derived from nearest-neighbor analysis of the CIF coordinates, should be physically reasonable. Extremely high coordination numbers (e.g., >10) or abnormally low ones (e.g., linear 2-coordinate for large cations) may indicate unstable local environments. Unusual coordination geometries that do not match the expected preference of the cation size and electronic configuration reduce the likelihood of successful synthesis.

def rule_02(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return np.mean([len(row)<=2 or len(row)>10 for row in shell(s)])
R03 Magnitude by which the nominal hard-sphere free-volume fraction lies outside the member's 10–35% band

Packing efficiency and void fraction** – The void fraction (unoccupied volume in the unit cell) should fall within a moderate range (typically 10–35%). Excessively high void fractions (>50%) suggest the framework is not a true energy minimum, while near-zero voids indicate severe repulsive overlap. Reasonable packing implies a stable, synthesizable arrangement.

def rule_03(structure):

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    v=1.-sum(n*(4*math.pi/3)*cov(e.symbol)**3 for e,n in s.composition.element_composition.items())/s.volume
    return max(.10-v,v-.35,0.)
R04 Largest fractional compression relative to the sum of the two tabulated covalent radii

No atom–atom overlaps or unphysically short non-bonded contacts.** All interatomic distances in the CIF must exceed the sum of van der Waals radii (for non-bonded pairs) or covalent radii (for bonded pairs). Any pair of atoms closer than their respective van der Waals or covalent radius sum signals steric clash or electron-density overlap, an immediate red flag that the structure is geometrically impossible and cannot correspond to a real, synthesizable phase.

def rule_04(structure):

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shortest_translation(s):
        lat=s.lattice
        upper=min(lat.abc)
        pts=lat.get_points_in_sphere([[0,0,0]],[0,0,0],upper+1e-6)
        return min(float(q[1]) for q in pts if q[1]>1e-8)

    def contacts(s,mode):

        sym=symbols(s)
        rad=[cov(x) for x in sym]
        D=s.distance_matrix
        trans=shortest_translation(s)
        values=[]
        for i in range(len(s)):
            if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[i]).is_metal: continue
            values.append(trans/(2*rad[i]))
            for j in range(i):
                if mode=="same" and sym[i]!=sym[j]: continue
                if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[j]).is_metal: continue
                values.append(float(D[i,j])/(rad[i]+rad[j]))
        return values

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return max(0.,1.-min(contacts(s,"all")))
R05 Mean per-site angular strain (degrees): nearest common ideal pair angle; a site without angles contributes zero accumulated distortion

Bond angles around each coordination polyhedron should approximate ideal values (e.g., 180°/90° for octahedral, 109.5° for tetrahedral). Systematic, large angular distortions (>15–20°) across multiple polyhedra indicate cumulative geometric strain, which raises the internal energy of the lattice and makes the phase unlikely to nucleate and grow as a stable crystal during synthesis.

def rule_05(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    pen=[]
    for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)):
        unit=[s.lattice.get_cartesian_coords(s[j].frac_coords+np.asarray(t)-s[i].frac_coords)/d for j,t,d in row]
        vals_a=[np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip(np.dot(a,b),-1.,1.))) for a,b in itertools.combinations(unit,2)]
        pen.append(sum(min(abs(v-k) for k in (60.,90.,109.47122063449069,120.,180.)) for v in vals_a)/max(len(vals_a),1))
    return np.mean(pen)
R06 Independent lattice-metric parameters for the detected crystal system

(Space group symmetry) The space group and its complexity directly influence synthesizability. Simple, high-symmetry groups (e.g., Pm-3m, Im-3m, P63/mmc) imply fewer independent parameters and lower entropic barriers to ordering, making the structure more accessible under standard solid-state or hydrothermal conditions. Highly complex or low-symmetry groups (e.g., P1, P-1 with many independent atoms) often demand precise templating, slow nucleation, or extreme conditions, reducing the likelihood of reproducible synthesis.

def rule_06(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    system=SpacegroupAnalyzer(s,symprec=0.01).get_crystal_system()
    return {"triclinic":6,"monoclinic":4,"orthorhombic":3,"tetragonal":2,"trigonal":2,"hexagonal":2,"cubic":1}[system]
R07 Fraction of sites with a common fourfold or sixfold first coordination shell; no prototype database

Coordination polyhedra and coordination numbers: Common, well-known coordination geometries (octahedral, tetrahedral, square-planar) are easier to realize synthetically because they arise from standard bonding preferences. Unusual or extreme coordination numbers (e.g., >10 for a small cation, or very low coordination for a highly charged ion) require precise control of ligand environment and may indicate a kinetically trapped, non-thermodynamic structure.

def rule_07(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return np.mean([len(row) in (4,6) for row in shell(s)])
R08 Mean absolute deviation in angstrom of first-shell lengths from additive covalent radii

Bond length plausibility** – All interatomic distances in the CIF should fall within ±0.2 Å of expected values derived from Pauling ionic radii or covalent radii tables. Unreasonably short bonds imply orbital overlap catastrophes, while excessively long bonds indicate no meaningful bonding interaction, both signaling a physically unrealizable structure.

def rule_08(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    rad=[cov(x) for x in symbols(s)]
    return np.mean([abs(d-rad[i]-rad[j]) for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)) for j,t,d in row])
R09 Maximum within-element spread in empirical signed site bond-valence sums, or in charge-sharing site strengths if elemental empirical parameters are missing

(The bond valence sum spread for a given element across all its crystallographic sites should be small. A wide spread indicates that the same cation occupies very different chemical environments, which is a hallmark of metastable or kinetically trapped structures that are difficult to obtain reproducibly.)

def rule_09(structure):

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    def nominal_assignment(s):

        am=s.composition.element_composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        sy=sorted(am)
        if len(sy)==1: return {sy[0]:0}
        xs={k:electr(k) for k in sy}; mean=sum(am[k]*xs[k] for k in sy)/sum(am.values())
        states={0.:(0.,0.,0.,())}
        for k in sy:
            nxt={}
            common=vals(k,False)
            available=tuple(sorted(set(Element(k).oxidation_states)|set(common)))
            for net,(p,u,a,qs) in states.items():
                for q in available:
                    key=round(net+am[k]*q,8)
                    score=(p+am[k]*max(0.,q*(xs[k]-mean)),u+(0. if q in common else am[k]),a+am[k]*abs(q),qs+(q,))
                    if key not in nxt or score<nxt[key]: nxt[key]=score
            states=nxt
        net=min(states,key=lambda k:(abs(k),states[k]))
        return dict(zip(sy,states[net][3]))

    def topological_valence(s):

        assignment=nominal_assignment(s)
        occ=np.asarray([site.species.num_atoms for site in s])
        q=np.asarray([sum(v*assignment[e.symbol] for e,v in site.species.items())/o for site,o in zip(s,occ)])
        incoming=np.zeros(len(s))
        if not (np.any(q>0) and np.any(q<0)): return incoming
        distances=s.distance_matrix
        minima=np.array([np.min(distances[i,np.where(q*qi<0)[0]]) if qi!=0 else 0. for i,qi in enumerate(q)])
        cutoff=1.20*float(max(minima))+1e-6
        ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
        rows=[[] for site in s]
        for i,j,d in zip(ii,jj,dd):
            if q[i]*q[j]<0 and 1e-8<d<=1.20*minima[i]+1e-7: rows[int(i)].append(int(j))
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if not row:
                if q[i]!=0: raise ValueError("Nominal ionic site has no opposite neighbor")
                continue
            den=sum(occ[j] for j in row)
            for j in row: incoming[j]-=q[i]*occ[i]/den
        return incoming

    def bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        if any(el.symbol not in params for el in st.composition.element_composition.elements):
            return topological_valence(st)
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    b=bvs(s)
    groups=defaultdict(list)
    for sym,v in zip(symbols(s),b): groups[sym].append(v)
    return max(max(row)-min(row) for row in groups.values())
R10 Number of distinct elements in the composition

Fewer distinct elements in the composition (extracted from the CIF _chemical_formula_sum) correlate strongly with easier synthesis, as fewer elemental sources are needed, stoichiometric control is simpler, and the probability of unwanted secondary phases decreases. Structures with two or three elements (e.g., binary oxides) are far more readily synthesized than quaternary or quinary compounds, where precise co-precipitation or solid-state mixing of multiple precursors becomes a critical bottleneck.

def rule_10(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return len(s.composition.element_composition.elements)
R11 Order of the crystallographic point group (distinct rotations, translations excluded)

High-symmetry space groups (e.g., cubic, hexagonal) with higher point groups indicate thermodynamically stable, energetically favorable arrangements. Materials crystallizing in high-symmetry settings are more likely to form spontaneously under conventional synthesis conditions. Low-symmetry or low-number space groups may signal metastable or kinetically trapped phases that are difficult to access via standard solid-state, hydrothermal, or flux methods, making symmetry a primary filter for synthesizability.

def rule_11(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    ds=SpacegroupAnalyzer(s,symprec=0.01).get_symmetry_dataset()
    return len(set(tuple(map(int,r.flatten())) for r in ds.rotations))
R12 Largest sitewise coefficient of variation of first-shell bond lengths: local radial distortion, not a chemical assignment

Coordination geometry validity. The coordination polyhedra around each cation, determined from CIF connectivity, must be geometrically and electronically reasonable: d⁰ and d¹⁰ cations tolerate a wide range, whereas Jahn–Teller-active ions (e.g., Cu²⁺, Mn³⁺) demand characteristic distortions. Anomalous coordination numbers or impossible geometries (e.g., 3-fold coordinated alkali metal in a dense oxide) suggest non-synthesizability.

def rule_12(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return max(np.std([d for j,t,d in row])/np.mean([d for j,t,d in row]) for row in shell(s))
R13 Fraction of all sites in explicitly enumerated tetrahedral or octahedral oxide building blocks (coordination and neighbor elements)

The structure should be composed of well-known, stable polyhedral building blocks such as SiO₄/PO₄ tetrahedra, MO₆ octahedra, or common chains and layers. Structures built from unrecognized or highly unusual polyhedral connections that do not correspond to established motifs are significantly less likely to be synthesizable.

def rule_13(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    sy=symbols(s); tally=0
    for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)):
        if not all(sy[j]=="O" for j,t,d in row): continue
        if (len(row)==4 and sy[i] in ("Si","P","B","Al")) or (len(row)==6 and sy[i] in ("Al","Ti","Mo","Re","Zr")): tally+=1
    return tally/len(s)
R14 Multiplicity-weighted mean number of equivalent sites per crystallographic orbit

Crystal symmetry serves as a proxy for thermodynamic stability. Higher-symmetry space groups (e.g., cubic, hexagonal) generally correspond to lower-energy, more isotropic bonding environments that are easier to nucleate and grow. Very low-symmetry structures (triclinic, P1) often represent local kinetic minima or defect motifs and are harder to obtain as pure, single-phase products. Symmetry also simplifies the stoichiometric and geometric constraints required during synthesis.

def rule_14(structure):

    def asym(s):
        ds=SpacegroupAnalyzer(s,symprec=0.01).get_symmetry_dataset()
        if ds is None: raise ValueError("Symmetry not resolved")
        return Counter(map(int,ds.equivalent_atoms))

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    mult=asym(s)
    return sum(n*n for n in mult.values())/len(s)
R15 Fraction of sites with mixed species or incomplete occupancy; full substitutional disorder counts but is not a vacancy

Fraction of atomic sites with partial or disordered occupancies (occupancy <1.0) in the CIF. High disorder (>20% of sites partially occupied, multiple scattering sites, or unresolved positional disorder) often reflects kinetic trapping during crystal growth. Such structures are reproducible with low yield and poor phase purity, indicating the material is marginal or difficult to synthesize in bulk.

def rule_15(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return np.mean([len(x.species)>1 or abs(x.species.num_atoms-1.)>1e-8 for x in s])
R16 Bounded multicomponent crystal-consistency score.

The assigned oxidation states should correspond to common, well-characterized ionic or covalent states of the constituent elements (e.g., Fe²⁺/Fe³⁺, Cu⁺/Cu²⁺, Ti³⁺/Ti⁴⁺). Exotic or uncommon oxidation states (e.g., Mo⁰, W¹⁺, Cr⁵⁺) require extreme redox conditions, specialized reductants/oxidants, or inert-atmosphere processing, drastically limiting practical synthesizability.

def rule_16(structure):
    def _signed_site_bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals
    _missing_attr = []
    struct = structure.copy()

    if len(struct) == 0:
        return float('nan')

    comp = struct.composition
    reduced = comp.reduced_composition
    n_elements = len(reduced)

    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(struct, symprec=1e-5)
        sg_number = sga.get_space_group_number()
        ptg_ops = sga.get_point_group_operations()
        n_point_ops = len(ptg_ops)
        n_independent = float(len(struct)) / n_point_ops if n_point_ops > 0 else float(len(struct))
    except Exception:
        return float('nan')

    try:
        cnn = CrystalNN()
        cn_list = []
        neighbor_dists = []
        for i in range(len(struct)):
            nbrs = cnn.get_nn_info(struct, i)
            cn_list.append(len(nbrs))
            neighbor_dists.append([float(np.linalg.norm(n['site'].coords - struct[i].coords)) for n in nbrs])
    except Exception:
        return float('nan')

    oxi_states = []
    for site in struct:
        sp = site.specie
        if (getattr(sp, 'oxi_state', _missing_attr) is not _missing_attr) and sp.oxi_state is not None:
            oxi_states.append(float(sp.oxi_state))
        else:
            oxi_states.append(None)

    all_oxi = all(s is not None for s in oxi_states)
    charge_balance = sum(oxi_states) if all_oxi else None

    bvs_list = []
    bvs_computed = False
    try:
        bvs_list = [float(x) for x in _signed_site_bvs(struct)]
        bvs_computed = True
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not bvs_computed:
        for i in range(len(struct)):
            elem_i = struct[i].specie
            r_i = float(elem_i.atomic_radius)
            bvs_i = 0.0
            for j in range(len(struct)):
                if i == j:
                    continue
                d_ij = struct.get_distance(i, j)
                if d_ij < 0.4 or d_ij > 4.5:
                    continue
                elem_j = struct[j].specie
                r_j = float(elem_j.atomic_radius)
                R0 = r_i + r_j
                b_param = 0.37
                s_ij = math.exp(-(d_ij - R0) / b_param)
                bvs_i += s_ij
            bvs_list.append(bvs_i)
        bvs_computed = True

    if not bvs_computed or len(bvs_list) != len(struct):
        return float('nan')

    total_atom_vol = 0.0
    for site in struct:
        r = float(site.specie.atomic_radius)
        total_atom_vol += (4.0 / 3.0) * math.pi * r ** 3
    cell_vol = struct.volume
    if cell_vol <= 0:
        return float('nan')
    packing_fraction = total_atom_vol / cell_vol
    void_fraction = 1.0 - packing_fraction

    min_dist = float('inf')
    for i in range(len(struct)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(struct)):
            d = struct.get_distance(i, j)
            if d < min_dist:
                min_dist = d
    if min_dist < 0.4:
        return float('nan')

    partial_occ_count = 0
    for site in struct:
        if sum(site.species.values()) < 0.90:
            partial_occ_count += 1

    eah = struct.properties.get('energy_above_hull', None)
    bg = struct.properties.get('band_gap', None)

    score = 1.0

    if charge_balance is not None:
        if abs(charge_balance) > 0.01:
            return 0.0
    else:
        score *= 0.5

    if all_oxi:
        non_int = sum(1 for s in oxi_states if abs(s - round(s)) > 0.01)
        if non_int > 0:
            score *= max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.25 * non_int / len(oxi_states))

    if all_oxi:
        devs = []
        for bvs, oxi in zip(bvs_list, oxi_states):
            expected = abs(oxi)
            if expected > 0.01:
                devs.append(abs(bvs - expected) / expected)
        if devs:
            mean_dev = sum(devs) / len(devs)
            score *= math.exp(-2.5 * mean_dev)

    cn_bad = sum(1 for cn in cn_list if cn < 1 or cn > 14)
    if cn_bad > 0:
        score *= max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.15 * cn_bad / len(cn_list))

    if packing_fraction < 0.10 or packing_fraction > 0.92:
        score *= 0.15
    elif packing_fraction < 0.20 or packing_fraction > 0.82:
        score *= 0.50
    elif packing_fraction < 0.28 or packing_fraction > 0.75:
        score *= 0.75

    sym_factor = min(n_point_ops / 48.0, 1.0)
    score *= (0.35 + 0.65 * sym_factor)

    if n_independent > 100.0:
        score *= 0.15
    elif n_independent > 50.0:
        score *= 0.35
    elif n_independent > 25.0:
        score *= 0.60
    elif n_independent > 12.0:
        score *= 0.80

    if n_elements > 10:
        score *= 0.15
    elif n_elements > 7:
        score *= 0.35
    elif n_elements > 5:
        score *= 0.55
    elif n_elements > 3:
        score *= 0.75

    if eah is not None:
        if eah > 1.5:
            score *= 0.05
        elif eah > 1.0:
            score *= 0.15
        elif eah > 0.5:
            score *= 0.35
        elif eah > 0.1:
            score *= 0.60
        elif eah > 0.0:
            score *= 0.85

    if bg is not None:
        if bg < -0.05 or bg > 15.0:
            score *= 0.25
        elif bg < 0.0:
            score *= 0.50

    if partial_occ_count > 0:
        frac_partial = partial_occ_count / len(struct)
        score *= max(0.0, 1.0 - 1.5 * frac_partial)

    overlap_violations = 0
    for i in range(len(struct)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(struct)):
            d = struct.get_distance(i, j)
            r_sum = float(struct[i].specie.atomic_radius) + float(struct[j].specie.atomic_radius)
            if d < 0.70 * r_sum:
                overlap_violations += 1
    if overlap_violations > 0:
        score *= max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.1 * overlap_violations)

    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))
R17 Hard-sphere interstitial volume fraction with a common radius scale reduced only when needed to prevent overlap

(Free volume / void fraction: The ratio of unoccupied interstitial volume to total cell volume (calculated via Voronoi or hard-sphere models on the CIF) informs synthetic accessibility. Moderate voids (10–30%) accommodate guest species and facilitate ion migration during growth. Voids >50% or <5% can indicate a framework that requires specific templating agents or is so rigid it collapses without in-situ stabilization.)

def rule_17(structure):

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shortest_translation(s):
        lat=s.lattice
        upper=min(lat.abc)
        pts=lat.get_points_in_sphere([[0,0,0]],[0,0,0],upper+1e-6)
        return min(float(q[1]) for q in pts if q[1]>1e-8)

    def contacts(s,mode):

        sym=symbols(s)
        rad=[cov(x) for x in sym]
        D=s.distance_matrix
        trans=shortest_translation(s)
        values=[]
        for i in range(len(s)):
            if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[i]).is_metal: continue
            values.append(trans/(2*rad[i]))
            for j in range(i):
                if mode=="same" and sym[i]!=sym[j]: continue
                if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[j]).is_metal: continue
                values.append(float(D[i,j])/(rad[i]+rad[j]))
        return values

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    scale=min(1.,min(contacts(s,"all")))
    filled=sum(n*4*math.pi*(scale*cov(e.symbol))**3/3 for e,n in s.composition.element_composition.items())/s.volume
    return 1.-filled
R18 Fraction of first-shell contacts within 15% of the covalent-radius sum

All bond lengths must fall within ±15% of expected values derived from ionic/covalent radius additivity or known analogues. Bonds that are significantly too long suggest weak, unstable interactions, while bonds that are too short indicate impossible compression. Out-of-range distances flag structurally implausible entries unlikely to be obtained as stable phases.

def rule_18(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shellratios(s):
        rad=[cov(x) for x in symbols(s)]
        return [d/(rad[i]+rad[j]) for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)) for j,t,d in row]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    q=np.asarray(shellratios(s))
    return np.mean(np.abs(q-1.)<=.15)
R19 Mean magnitude outside the stated 0.8–1.3 first-shell covalent-radius window

All interatomic distances should fall within a physically reasonable range, typically 0.8–1.3 times the sum of the relevant ionic or covalent radii. Bonds that are too short indicate impossible orbital overlap or steric clash, while excessively long bonds suggest the framework lacks sufficient cohesive energy to hold together, both pointing to a non-synthesizable structure.

def rule_19(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
