R20 Absolute net charge per site of independently selected BVS-nearest common valences; not measured oxidation states

Charge balance and oxidation state compatibility: All formal oxidation states must be chemically plausible and consistent with known valences, and the structure must satisfy overall charge neutrality. Structures requiring unusual or unsupported oxidation states (e.g., Fe⁰ in a simple oxide, or C⁴⁻ in an inorganic framework) indicate the required redox chemistry may be unachievable, sharply reducing synthesizability.

def rule_20(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def nearest_states(s,b):
        mono=len(s.composition.elements)==1
        ref=[]
        for site,obs in zip(s,b):
            z=0.
            for el,occ in site.species.items():
                q=min(vals(el.symbol,mono),key=lambda x:(abs(obs-x),abs(x),x))
                z+=occ*q
            ref.append(z/sum(site.species.values()))
        return np.asarray(ref,dtype=float)

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    def nominal_assignment(s):

        am=s.composition.element_composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        sy=sorted(am)
        if len(sy)==1: return {sy[0]:0}
        xs={k:electr(k) for k in sy}; mean=sum(am[k]*xs[k] for k in sy)/sum(am.values())
        states={0.:(0.,0.,0.,())}
        for k in sy:
            nxt={}
            common=vals(k,False)
            available=tuple(sorted(set(Element(k).oxidation_states)|set(common)))
            for net,(p,u,a,qs) in states.items():
                for q in available:
                    key=round(net+am[k]*q,8)
                    score=(p+am[k]*max(0.,q*(xs[k]-mean)),u+(0. if q in common else am[k]),a+am[k]*abs(q),qs+(q,))
                    if key not in nxt or score<nxt[key]: nxt[key]=score
            states=nxt
        net=min(states,key=lambda k:(abs(k),states[k]))
        return dict(zip(sy,states[net][3]))

    def topological_valence(s):

        assignment=nominal_assignment(s)
        occ=np.asarray([site.species.num_atoms for site in s])
        q=np.asarray([sum(v*assignment[e.symbol] for e,v in site.species.items())/o for site,o in zip(s,occ)])
        incoming=np.zeros(len(s))
        if not (np.any(q>0) and np.any(q<0)): return incoming
        distances=s.distance_matrix
        minima=np.array([np.min(distances[i,np.where(q*qi<0)[0]]) if qi!=0 else 0. for i,qi in enumerate(q)])
        cutoff=1.20*float(max(minima))+1e-6
        ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
        rows=[[] for site in s]
        for i,j,d in zip(ii,jj,dd):
            if q[i]*q[j]<0 and 1e-8<d<=1.20*minima[i]+1e-7: rows[int(i)].append(int(j))
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if not row:
                if q[i]!=0: raise ValueError("Nominal ionic site has no opposite neighbor")
                continue
            den=sum(occ[j] for j in row)
            for j in row: incoming[j]-=q[i]*occ[i]/den
        return incoming

    def bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        if any(el.symbol not in params for el in st.composition.element_composition.elements):
            return topological_valence(st)
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    b=bvs(s);z=nearest_states(s,b)
    return abs(np.average(z,weights=[x.species.num_atoms for x in s]))
R21 Repeat probability of chemical coordination fingerprints within the unit cell; recurrence, not experimental precedent

(The presence of well-known, recurring structural motifs—such as spinel, perovskite, zeolite frameworks, or rutile-type substructures—supports synthesizability because these building blocks are proven to assemble under established chemical conditions. Structures composed entirely of unprecedented or highly unusual coordination polyhedra lack precedent, suggesting unknown or unachievable synthetic pathways.)

def rule_21(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    g=shell(s); sy=symbols(s)
    cnt=Counter((sy[i],tuple(sorted(sy[j] for j,t,d in row))) for i,row in enumerate(g))
    return sum((v/len(s))**2 for v in cnt.values())
R22 Smallest absolute net charge per atom with one tabulated common integer valence per element; mixed valence not assigned

(Charge neutrality must be satisfied: the sum of all cationic oxidation states must exactly balance the total anionic charge. A CIF describing a non-neutral, non-compensated composition represents an impossible bulk phase, immediately ruling out synthesizability. Verification of this is the most fundamental check on any proposed inorganic structure.)

def rule_22(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def spectrum(s):

        amounts=s.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        result={0.:1}
        for sym,n in sorted(amounts.items()):
            nxt=defaultdict(int)
            for prev,mult in result.items():
                for z in vals(sym,len(amounts)==1):
                    nxt[round(prev+n*z,8)]+=mult
            result=nxt
        return result

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return min(abs(z) for z in spectrum(s))/s.composition.num_atoms
R23 Mean local mismatch between a central covalent radius and the cavity implied by its neighbors; geometric proxy, not a Shannon ratio

Ionic radii of cations must fall within the Goldschmidt tolerance or radius-ratio limits for their coordination polyhedra. Severe radius mismatches (cations significantly smaller or larger than the polyhedral cavity) create excessive lattice strain, making the structure metastable or unobtainable under equilibrium synthesis conditions.

def rule_23(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    rr=[cov(x) for x in symbols(s)]
    return np.mean([abs(np.mean([d-rr[j] for j,t,d in row])-rr[i])/rr[i] for i,row in enumerate(shell(s))])
R24 Count of symmetry-inequivalent occupied sites in the recorded unit cell

The number of atoms in the asymmetric unit should be small. A large asymmetric unit indicates a complex, unique structural motif with many independent degrees of freedom, making reproducible synthesis difficult. Structures with a few atoms in the asymmetric unit can form through simple diffusion-limited or self-assembly pathways under moderate conditions.

def rule_24(structure):

    def asym(s):
        ds=SpacegroupAnalyzer(s,symprec=0.01).get_symmetry_dataset()
        if ds is None: raise ValueError("Symmetry not resolved")
        return Counter(map(int,ds.equivalent_atoms))

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return len(asym(s))
R25 Largest fraction matching one simple local prototype signature (tetrahedral AB, octahedral AB, eightfold AB or close-packed elemental); not a database match

The structure should show measurable similarity (via fingerprint vectors, connectivity, or topological matching) to at least one known, experimentally synthesized phase in the same or adjacent chemical family. Absence of any structural precedent in a well-explored composition space is a strong negative indicator of synthesizability.

def rule_25(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    g=shell(s); sy=symbols(s); counts=Counter()
    for i,row in enumerate(g):
        nn=min(d for j,t,d in row)
        sig=[sy[j] for j,t,d in row if d<=1.08*nn+1e-7]
        if not sig or len(set(sig))!=1: continue
        if sig[0]!=sy[i] and len(sig) in (4,6,8): counts[(len(sig),True)]+=1
        elif sig[0]==sy[i] and len(sig) in (4,8,12): counts[(len(sig),False)]+=1
    return max([0]+list(counts.values()))/len(s)
R26 RMS fractional departure of first-shell contact lengths from covalent-radius sums

Bond lengths in the CIF should fall within 10–15% of expected ionic or covalent radii sums. Significant deviations indicate extreme compressive or tensile strain in the lattice, implying the structure exists far from its equilibrium energy minimum and would require extraordinary synthesis conditions such as extreme pressure or metastable precursors to realize.

def rule_26(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shellratios(s):
        rad=[cov(x) for x in symbols(s)]
        return [d/(rad[i]+rad[j]) for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)) for j,t,d in row]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(np.asarray(shellratios(s))-1.)))
R27 Mean electronegativity contrast across the actual first-neighbor contacts, proxy for pair chemistry, not evidence of known phases

(The elemental composition should be composed of elements with well-known binary or ternary bonding tendencies and compatible electronegativities. Combinations that fall within established chemical systematics (e.g., known ABO₃, MX₂ families) are more synthesizable. Unusual element pairings with no precedent in the literature, or extreme electronegativity mismatches, suggest the phase may exist only under exotic conditions or as a metastable intermediate.)

def rule_27(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    X=[electr(x) for x in symbols(s)]
    return np.mean([abs(X[i]-X[j]) for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)) for j,t,d in row])
R28 Atomic fraction of elements with no stable isotopes; a handling/availability indicator, not a precursor price or a route

Elemental composition in the CIF should consist of commonly available, non-radioactive elements with established synthetic precedents. The presence of extremely rare, highly electropositive, or transuranic elements drastically reduces synthesizability, as appropriate precursors and reaction conditions become impractical. A material built from abundant elements (Si, O, Al, Fe, transition metals) with known binary/ternary oxide or chalcogenide chemistry is far more likely to be accessible via solid-state, sol-gel, or hydrothermal routes.

def rule_28(structure):

    def parts(s):
        comp=s.composition.element_composition
        return [(el,float(n)/comp.num_atoms) for el,n in comp.items()]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return sum(p for e,p in parts(s) if e.Z in (43,61) or e.Z>=83)
R29 Number of reduced formula units in the stored unit cell (pymatgen reduced-composition convention)

(A small unit cell containing few formula units (low Z) indicates a simpler structural motif, which is generally easier to nucleate, grow, and reproduce under standard solid-state or hydrothermal conditions. Very large unit cells (e.g., >200 atoms) often reflect complex cation ordering, superstructure formation, or long-period modulations that demand narrow compositional windows and precise thermal histories, reducing practical synthesizability.)

def rule_29(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    comp,factor=s.composition.element_composition.get_reduced_composition_and_factor()
    return factor
R30 Component-size-weighted rank of periodic translation cycles in a geometric first-shell network (0D–3D)

Structural dimensionality and connectivity: Fully connected 3D frameworks with high topological complexity (e.g., interpenetrating subnets, unusual net types) are harder to synthesize than discrete clusters, 1D chains, or simple 2D layers. The connectivity pattern governs the number of competing bond-forming events during nucleation, directly affecting synthetic feasibility.

def rule_30(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def dimension(s):

        g=shell(s)
        adj=[[] for row in g]
        for i,row in enumerate(g):
            for j,t,d in row:
                tr=np.array(t,dtype=int)
                adj[i].append((j,tr)); adj[j].append((i,-tr))
        pos={}; weighted=0
        for start in range(len(s)):
            if start in pos: continue
            pos[start]=np.zeros(3,dtype=int); stack=[start]; size=0; cycles=[]
            while stack:
                i=stack.pop();size+=1
                for j,t in adj[i]:
                    p=pos[i]+t
                    if j not in pos: pos[j]=p;stack.append(j)
                    else:
                        v=p-pos[j]
                        if np.any(v): cycles.append(v)
            rank=int(np.linalg.matrix_rank(np.asarray(cycles))) if cycles else 0
            weighted+=size*rank
        return weighted/len(s)

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return dimension(s)
R31 Crystallographic information per cell: number of sites times Shannon entropy of orbit multiplicities (bits/cell)

Structural complexity (unique atom count, space group, unit cell size): Simpler structures with fewer unique atom sites, higher symmetry, and smaller unit cells are significantly easier to synthesize. A CIF with many disordered sites, low symmetry (P1), or a large unit cell introduces many degrees of freedom that must be simultaneously satisfied, making reproducible synthesis far more challenging.

def rule_31(structure):

    def asym(s):
        ds=SpacegroupAnalyzer(s,symprec=0.01).get_symmetry_dataset()
        if ds is None: raise ValueError("Symmetry not resolved")
        return Counter(map(int,ds.equivalent_atoms))

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    mult=asym(s);n=len(s)
    return -sum(v*math.log2(v/n) for v in mult.values())
R32 Mean site occupancy deficit

High atomic displacement parameters (U_iso or U_eq values > ~0.08 Å²) and partial site occupancies (well below 1.0) reveal significant positional or compositional disorder. Disordered structures often form only under metastable conditions, with kinetic trapping rather than thermodynamic driving force. Such features in a CIF flag that the phase may be a byproduct, a surface phase, or a product requiring very slow, carefully controlled cooling rather than a robust bulk phase.

def rule_32(structure):

    n_sites = len(structure)
    if n_sites == 0:
        return float('nan')

    total_occu = 0.0
    for site in structure:
        occu = sum(site.species.values())
        if occu is None:
            return float('nan')
        total_occu += float(occu)

    mean_occu = total_occu / n_sites
    return 1.0 - mean_occu
R33 Fraction of one-common-valence-per-element assignments that exactly charge-balance the composition (not a posterior probability)

Overall charge balance and electroneutrality: Summing all cation and anion charges from the CIF's chemical formula and assigned oxidation states must yield a net-zero cell. A charged unit cell (e.g., net +1 or −2 per formula unit) without a compensating counter-ion indicates an incomplete or incorrect structure, precluding it from representing a real, synthesizable compound.

def rule_33(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def spectrum(s):

        amounts=s.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        result={0.:1}
        for sym,n in sorted(amounts.items()):
            nxt=defaultdict(int)
            for prev,mult in result.items():
                for z in vals(sym,len(amounts)==1):
                    nxt[round(prev+n*z,8)]+=mult
            result=nxt
        return result

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    ways=spectrum(s)
    return ways.get(0.,0)/sum(ways.values())
R34 Unclipped nominal hard-sphere packing fraction from fixed covalent radii; values above one indicate overlapping nominal spheres

(The packing fraction, derived from atomic volumes relative to unit cell volume, should be reasonable (typically 0.5–0.8 for dense inorganic solids). Very low packing fractions suggest excessive void space, implying a high-temperature or metastable framework that may collapse under ambient conditions. Very high packing fractions beyond physical limits indicate unphysical structures. Normal packing efficiency supports thermodynamic stability and practical synthesizability.)

def rule_34(structure):

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return sum(n*(4*math.pi/3)*cov(e.symbol)**3 for e,n in s.composition.element_composition.items())/s.volume
R35 Crystal mass density, in grams per cubic centimeter

The calculated density (mass of unit cell divided by unit cell volume) should fall within a physically reasonable range for the constituent elements (typically 1–22 g/cm³ for common inorganics). Anomalous densities (e.g., >30 g/cm³ for a light-element compound) or unreasonably large void fractions suggest the structure may not pack into a real, stable crystalline phase.

def rule_35(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return s.density
R36 Archived energy above hull (eV per atom)

Thermodynamic stability via formation energy: A negative enthalpy of formation relative to constituent elements indicates the compound is thermodynamically favorable. Materials far above the convex hull in energy are unlikely to form under standard synthesis conditions. CIF-derived structural data combined with computational formation energies (e.g., DFT) allows assessment of whether the structure represents a local or global energy minimum, a prerequisite for reproducible synthesis.

def rule_36(structure):

    try:
        eah = structure.properties['energy_above_hull']
    except (KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError):
        return float('nan')

    if eah is None:
        return float('nan')

    try:
        val = float(eah)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return float('nan')

    if math.isnan(val) or math.isinf(val):
        return float('nan')

    return val
R37 Fraction of occupied sites that are symmetry-inequivalent in the supplied cell

The number of independent (Wyckoff) atomic sites should be modest relative to the total atom count. A small number of unique positions implies a simple, highly symmetric framework with fewer constraints to satisfy during nucleation and growth. Many unique sites (e.g., > 15 independent positions) demand precise positional ordering, increasing kinetic barriers and sensitivity to synthesis conditions.

def rule_37(structure):

    def asym(s):
        ds=SpacegroupAnalyzer(s,symprec=0.01).get_symmetry_dataset()
        if ds is None: raise ValueError("Symmetry not resolved")
        return Counter(map(int,ds.equivalent_atoms))

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return len(asym(s))/len(s)
R38 All-site tetrahedral/octahedral shape participation: angular agreement with the ideal ligand-pair angles; no ionic-radius lookup

(Presence of known structural subunits and motifs) If the CIF structure can be decomposed into well-known polyhedral building blocks (SiO₄, TiO₆, AlO₆, etc.) and recognized archetypal arrangements, it is more likely synthesizable. Familiar motifs correspond to established synthetic pathways, whereas novel, unrecognizable topologies raise uncertainty about viable synthetic routes and phase purity.

def rule_38(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    scores=[]
    for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)):
        n=len(row)
        if n not in (4,6):
            scores.append(0.);continue
        u=[s.lattice.get_cartesian_coords(s[j].frac_coords+np.asarray(t)-s[i].frac_coords)/d for j,t,d in row]
        a=np.asarray(u)
        c=np.sort(np.clip((a@a.T)[np.triu_indices(n,1)],-1.,1.))
        ref=np.array([-1/3.]*6 if n==4 else [-1.]*3+[0.]*12)
        scores.append(1.-np.sqrt(np.mean((c-ref)**2))/2.)
    return np.mean(scores)
R39 Minimum distance/sum-of-covalent-radii over distinct atoms in the infinite periodic crystal, including the nearest self image

(Absence of unphysical atomic overlaps and unreasonable voids), A CIF must show no unphysically close atom–atom contacts (less than the sum of van der Waals radii) and no excessively large empty voids unaccounted for by the known composition. Such geometric impossibilities indicate the structure is a computational artifact or misindexed unit cell, and the material cannot be isolated as a real crystalline phase.

def rule_39(structure):

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shortest_translation(s):
        lat=s.lattice
        upper=min(lat.abc)
        pts=lat.get_points_in_sphere([[0,0,0]],[0,0,0],upper+1e-6)
        return min(float(q[1]) for q in pts if q[1]>1e-8)

    def contacts(s,mode):

        sym=symbols(s)
        rad=[cov(x) for x in sym]
        D=s.distance_matrix
        trans=shortest_translation(s)
        values=[]
        for i in range(len(s)):
            if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[i]).is_metal: continue
            values.append(trans/(2*rad[i]))
            for j in range(i):
                if mode=="same" and sym[i]!=sym[j]: continue
                if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[j]).is_metal: continue
                values.append(float(D[i,j])/(rad[i]+rad[j]))
        return values

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return min(contacts(s,"all"))
R40 Axial-ratio-penalized atomic packing fraction

Unit cell volume and mass density** – A reasonable unit cell volume (typically < 10,000 Å³) and physically plausible mass density (consistent with constituent atomic masses) indicate a compact, stable lattice. Excessively large cells suggest complex frameworks or inclusion of solvent/voids that are difficult to reproduce in bulk synthesis, raising the probability that the structure is a metastable artifact.

def rule_40(structure):

    lattice = structure.lattice
    a = lattice.a
    b = lattice.b
    c = lattice.c
    alpha = math.radians(lattice.alpha)
    beta = math.radians(lattice.beta)
    gamma = math.radians(lattice.gamma)

    cos_a = math.cos(alpha)
    cos_b = math.cos(beta)
    cos_g = math.cos(gamma)
    sin_a = math.sin(alpha)
    sin_b = math.sin(beta)
    sin_g = math.sin(gamma)

    det = 1.0 - cos_a**2 - cos_b**2 - cos_g**2 + 2.0 * cos_a * cos_b * cos_g
    if det <= 0.0:
        return float('nan')

    V = a * b * c * math.sqrt(det)
    if V <= 0.0:
        return float('nan')

    comp = structure.composition
    if len(comp) == 0:
        return float('nan')

    reduced_comp = comp.reduced_composition
    n_atoms_formula = sum(reduced_comp.values())
    n_atoms_cell = len(structure)
    if n_atoms_formula == 0 or n_atoms_cell == 0:
        return float('nan')

    Z = n_atoms_cell / n_atoms_formula

    formula_weight = reduced_comp.weight
    if formula_weight is None or formula_weight <= 0.0:
        return float('nan')

    N_A = 6.02214076e23
    V_cm3 = V * 1.0e-24
    density = (Z * formula_weight) / (V_cm3 * N_A)

    V_per_Z = V / Z

    total_sphere_volume = 0.0
    for site in structure:
        elem = site.specie
        r = elem.atomic_radius
        if r is None or r <= 0.0:
            return float('nan')
        total_sphere_volume += (4.0 / 3.0) * math.pi * r ** 3

    apf = total_sphere_volume / V

    ratios = [a / b, b / c, a / c]
    axial_penalty = 1.0
    for ratio in ratios:
        if ratio > 3.0 or ratio < 1.0 / 3.0:
            axial_penalty = 0.5
            break

    result = apf * axial_penalty

    if not math.isfinite(result):
        return float('nan')

    return float(result)
R41 Inequivalent-site count times unit-cell formula units

The number of atoms in the asymmetric unit (Z') should be moderate. Structures with very high Z' (e.g., >30) imply extreme compositional complexity and multiple distinct sites, making reproducible synthesis highly challenging. Low Z' values (1–4) typically correspond to simple, well-defined structures that crystallize readily under predictable conditions.

def rule_41(structure):
    struct = structure.copy()

    n_atoms = len(struct)
    if n_atoms == 0:
        return float('nan')

    sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(struct)
    sym_ops = sga.get_symmetry_operations()

    frac_coords = [np.array(atom.frac_coords, dtype=float) for atom in struct]
    species_strs = [str(atom.specie) for atom in struct]

    tol = 1e-4

    def positions_equivalent(frac1, frac2):
        diff = frac1 - frac2
        return np.allclose(diff, np.round(diff), atol=tol)

    visited = [False] * n_atoms
    n_orbits = 0

    for i in range(n_atoms):
        if visited[i]:
            continue
        n_orbits += 1

        queue = [i]
        visited[i] = True
        qi = 0
        while qi < len(queue):
            j = queue[qi]
            qi += 1
            for op in sym_ops:
                new_frac = np.asarray(op.operate(frac_coords[j]), dtype=float)
                for k in range(n_atoms):
                    if visited[k]:
                        continue
                    if species_strs[k] != species_strs[j]:
                        continue
                    if positions_equivalent(new_frac, frac_coords[k]):
                        visited[k] = True
                        queue.append(k)

    Z_prime = n_orbits

    comp = struct.composition
    reduced_comp = comp.reduced_composition
    atoms_per_formula = sum(reduced_comp.values())
    total_atoms = sum(comp.values())

    if atoms_per_formula == 0:
        return float('nan')

    Z = total_atoms / atoms_per_formula

    return float(Z_prime * Z)
R42 Coordinate and symmetry consistency, overlap and order composite

The assigned space group must be fully consistent with the atomic coordinates, including all symmetry operations, special positions, and equivalent atoms. Violations of site symmetry, missing symmetry-related atoms, or incompatible space-group assignments indicate a flawed or fictitious structure. A crystallographically sound, symmetry-consistent model is essential for a real, synthesizable phase.

def rule_42(structure):
    try:
        struct = structure.copy()
        n_atoms = len(struct)
        if n_atoms == 0:
            return float('nan')

        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(struct, symprec=0.1)
        ops = sga.get_symmetry_operations()
        lattice = struct.lattice
        n_ops = len(ops)
        if n_ops == 0:
            return float('nan')

        frac_coords = np.array([s.frac_coords for s in struct])
        cart_coords = np.array([lattice.get_cartesian_coords(s.frac_coords) for s in struct])
        species_list = [s.specie for s in struct]

        max_dev = 0.0
        for i in range(n_atoms):
            for op in ops:
                new_frac = (op.rotation_matrix @ frac_coords[i] + op.translation_vector) % 1.0
                cart_new = lattice.get_cartesian_coords(new_frac)
                min_d = float('inf')
                for j in range(n_atoms):
                    if species_list[j] != species_list[i]:
                        continue
                    d = np.linalg.norm(cart_new - cart_coords[j])
                    if d < min_d:
                        min_d = d
                if min_d < float('inf'):
                    if min_d > max_dev:
                        max_dev = min_d
                else:

                    max_dev = max(max_dev, 10.0)

        wyckoff_mismatch = 0
        try:
            site_syms = sga.get_symmetry_dataset().site_symmetry_symbols
            for i in range(n_atoms):
                sym_str = site_syms[i]
                if sym_str != '1':
                    frac = frac_coords[i]
                    is_on_special = False
                    for c in frac:
                        for target in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                            if abs(c - target) < 0.02:
                                is_on_special = True
                                break
                        if is_on_special:
                            break
                    if not is_on_special:
                        wyckoff_mismatch += 1
        except Exception:
            wyckoff_mismatch = 0

        overlap_count = 0
        for i in range(n_atoms):
            r_i = species_list[i].atomic_radius
            for op in ops:
                new_frac = (op.rotation_matrix @ frac_coords[i] + op.translation_vector) % 1.0
                cart_new = lattice.get_cartesian_coords(new_frac)
                for j in range(n_atoms):
                    if j == i:
                        continue
                    r_j = species_list[j].atomic_radius
                    d = np.linalg.norm(cart_new - cart_coords[j])
                    if 0.0 < d < 0.35 * (r_i + r_j):
                        overlap_count += 1

        n_elements = len(set(species_list))
        complexity = float(n_elements) + float(n_atoms) / max(n_elements, 1)

        if complexity <= 3.0:

            order_mismatch = max(0.0, (4.0 - float(n_ops)) / 4.0)
        elif complexity <= 6.0:
            order_mismatch = max(0.0, (float(n_ops) - 8.0) / 8.0)
        else:
            order_mismatch = max(0.0, (float(n_ops) - 4.0) / 4.0)

        residual = max_dev + 0.1 * float(wyckoff_mismatch) + 0.1 * float(overlap_count) + 0.5 * order_mismatch

        return float(residual)

    except Exception:
        return float('nan')
R43 Site-occupancy-weighted fraction of H or low-coordinate alkali species (first-shell CN <= 4); candidate labile species, not an identification of trapped solvent

(Presence of volatile or labile species): CIF entries containing water, methanol, ammonia, or light alkali cations in channels or interlayer sites signal that the structure is often only accessible via hydrothermal, solvothermal, or ion-exchange routes. These species may be lost upon heating or in ambient air, making isolation of the as-synthesized phase difficult and limiting synthetic reproducibility.

def rule_43(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    weight=0.
    for site,row in zip(s,shell(s)):
        for el,occ in site.species.items():
            if el.symbol=="H" or (el.symbol in ("Li","Na","K","Rb","Cs") and len(row)<=4): weight+=occ
    return weight/s.composition.num_atoms
R44 Spread of Pauling electronegativity contrasts across first-shell contacts

Electronegativity compatibility and bond character: From the elements in the CIF formula, assess electronegativity differences between bonded pairs. Bonds should match expected ionic, covalent, or metallic character for the element pair (e.g., metal–O ionic, Si–O covalent). Structures forcing inappropriate bonding (e.g., highly covalent bonds across large electronegativity gaps, or ionic bonds between similar electronegativities) are unlikely to form under standard synthesis routes.

def rule_44(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    X=[electr(x) for x in symbols(s)]
    return np.std([abs(X[i]-X[j]) for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)) for j,t,d in row])
R45 Composition, stoichiometry, occupancy and formal-charge composite

Stoichiometric simplicity of the formula strongly correlates with ease of synthesis. Whole-number or simple fractional ratios (e.g., AB, AB₂, A₂B₃, A₃B₄) correspond to well-defined stoichiometric compounds accessible by balancing precursor mixtures with high accuracy. Complex or non-integer ratios (e.g., A₁.₀₃B₀.₉₇C₁.₁₂) indicate intrinsic non-stoichiometry, vacancy disorder, or site-mixing, all of which broaden the compositional window, complicate phase-pure isolation, and demand precise thermodynamic control during synthesis.

def rule_45(structure):
    try:
        comp = structure.composition
        n_elements = len(comp)
        if n_elements == 0:
            return float('nan')

        elem_score = min(1.0, max(0.0, (n_elements - 2) / 6.0))

        amounts = [float(amt) for amt in comp.values()]
        positive = [a for a in amounts if a > 0]
        if not positive:
            return float('nan')
        min_amt = min(positive)
        normalized = [a / min_amt for a in positive]

        def _stoich_complexity(x, tol=0.06):

            if abs(x - round(x)) < tol:
                return 0.0

            for d in (2, 3, 4):
                for n in range(1, 20):
                    if abs(x - n / d) < tol:
                        return 0.5
            return 1.0

        stoich_score = max(_stoich_complexity(v) for v in normalized)

        n_sites = len(structure)
        if n_sites == 0:
            return float('nan')
        n_defective = 0
        for site in structure:
            species = site.species
            total_occ = sum(occ for _, occ in species.items())
            if total_occ < 1.0 - 1e-4 or len(species) > 1:
                n_defective += 1
        occ_score = n_defective / n_sites

        std_ox = {
            'H': 1, 'Li': 1, 'Na': 1, 'K': 1, 'Rb': 1, 'Cs': 1,
            'Be': 2, 'Mg': 2, 'Ca': 2, 'Sr': 2, 'Ba': 2,
            'B': 3, 'Al': 3, 'Ga': 3, 'In': 3, 'Tl': 3,
            'Sc': 3, 'Y': 3, 'La': 3, 'Ce': 4, 'Pr': 3, 'Nd': 3,
            'Sm': 3, 'Eu': 3, 'Gd': 3, 'Tb': 3, 'Dy': 3, 'Ho': 3,
            'Er': 3, 'Tm': 3, 'Yb': 3, 'Lu': 3,
            'Ti': 4, 'Zr': 4, 'Hf': 4,
            'V': 5, 'Nb': 5, 'Ta': 5,
            'Cr': 3, 'Mo': 6, 'W': 6,
            'Mn': 4, 'Fe': 3, 'Co': 3, 'Ni': 2,
            'Cu': 2, 'Zn': 2, 'Cd': 2, 'Hg': 2,
            'Sn': 4, 'Pb': 4,
            'U': 6, 'Th': 4, 'Pa': 5, 'Np': 4, 'Pu': 4,
            'O': -2, 'F': -1, 'Cl': -1, 'Br': -1, 'I': -1,
            'S': -2, 'Se': -2, 'Te': -2,
            'N': -3, 'P': -3, 'As': -3,
            'C': -4, 'Si': 4, 'Ge': 4,
        }

        total_charge = 0.0
        total_abs_charge = 0.0
        all_known = True
        for el, amt in comp.items():
            sym = el.symbol
            if sym in std_ox:
                c = std_ox[sym] * float(amt)
                total_charge += c
                total_abs_charge += abs(c)
            else:
                all_known = False
                break

        if all_known and total_abs_charge > 0:
            charge_score = 0.0 if abs(total_charge) / total_abs_charge < 0.05 else 1.0
        else:
            charge_score = 0.5

        subunit_match = False
        if 'O' in comp:
            n_O = float(comp['O'])
            non_O = {el: float(amt) for el, amt in comp.items() if el.symbol != 'O'}
            n_non_O = len(non_O)

            if n_O > 0 and n_non_O > 0:
                ratios = [amt / n_O for amt in non_O.values()]

                if n_non_O == 1:
                    r = ratios[0]
                    targets_1 = [1.0, 0.5, 1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0, 0.25, 2.0, 3.0]
                    if any(abs(r - t) < 0.03 for t in targets_1):
                        subunit_match = True

                elif n_non_O == 2:
                    r1, r2 = ratios
                    target_pairs = [
                        (1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0),
                        (1.0 / 4.0, 0.5),
                        (0.5, 1.0 / 4.0),
                        (2.0 / 7.0, 2.0 / 7.0),
                        (1.0 / 4.0, 1.0 / 4.0),
                        (1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0),
                        (2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0),
                        (1.0 / 4.0, 3.0 / 4.0),
                        (3.0 / 4.0, 1.0 / 4.0),
                        (0.5, 0.5),
                    ]
                    for (a, b) in target_pairs:
                        if (abs(r1 - a) < 0.03 and abs(r2 - b) < 0.03) or \
                           (abs(r1 - b) < 0.03 and abs(r2 - a) < 0.03):
                            subunit_match = True
                            break

                elif n_non_O == 3:
                    r_vals = sorted(ratios)
                    targets_3 = [
                        (1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0),
                        (2.0 / 7.0, 2.0 / 7.0, 3.0 / 7.0),
                        (1.0 / 4.0, 1.0 / 4.0, 2.0 / 4.0),
                    ]
                    for t in targets_3:
                        if all(abs(r_vals[i] - t[i]) < 0.03 for i in range(3)):
                            subunit_match = True
                            break
        else:

            if n_elements == 2:
                vals = [float(a) for a in comp.values()]
                if vals[1] > 0:
                    ratio = vals[0] / vals[1]
                    simple_ratios = [1, 2, 3, 4, 0.5, 1.0 / 3.0, 0.25, 1.5, 2.5, 3.5, 0.75, 4.0]
                    if any(abs(ratio - s) < 0.03 for s in simple_ratios):
                        subunit_match = True
            elif n_elements == 3:
                vals = sorted(float(a) for a in comp.values())
                if vals[2] > 0:
                    r = [v / vals[2] for v in vals]
                    ternary_targets = [
                        (1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0, 1.0),
                        (1.0 / 4.0, 0.5, 1.0),
                        (0.5, 0.5, 1.0),
                    ]
                    for t in ternary_targets:
                        if all(abs(r[i] - t[i]) < 0.03 for i in range(3)):
                            subunit_match = True
                            break

        subunit_score = 0.0 if subunit_match else 1.0

        w = [0.25, 0.20, 0.20, 0.20, 0.15]
        score = (w[0] * elem_score +
                 w[1] * stoich_score +
                 w[2] * occ_score +
                 w[3] * charge_score +
                 w[4] * subunit_score)

        return float(score)

    except Exception:
        return float('nan')
R46 Lattice aspect, radius-to-volume, angle and edge consistency composite

(Lattice parameters should show no extreme anisotropy (c/a ratio within ~0.5–3 for most classes) and unit-cell dimensions should be within typical 3–20 Å ranges. Highly anisotropic or abnormally large unit cells often reflect shear instability, poor stacking tolerance, or phase-association artifacts, all of which reduce the likelihood of reproducible single-phase synthesis.)

def rule_46(structure):
    _cov_table = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}

    struct = structure.copy()

    lattice = struct.lattice
    a, b, c = lattice.a, lattice.b, lattice.c
    alpha, beta, gamma = lattice.alpha, lattice.beta, lattice.gamma
    volume = lattice.volume
    n_atoms = len(struct)

    if n_atoms == 0 or volume <= 0 or min(a, b, c) <= 0:
        return float('nan')

    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(struct)
        crystal_system = sga.get_crystal_system()
    except Exception:
        return float('nan')

    edges = [a, b, c]
    edge_ratio = max(edges) / min(edges)
    edge_anisotropy = edge_ratio - 1.0

    expected_vol = 0.0
    for site in struct:
        el = site.specie
        try:
            r = _cov_table.get((el).symbol)
        except Exception:
            return float('nan')
        if r is None or r <= 0:
            return float('nan')
        expected_vol += (4.0 / 3.0) * math.pi * r ** 3

    if expected_vol <= 0:
        return float('nan')

    vol_ratio = volume / expected_vol
    if vol_ratio <= 0:
        return float('nan')
    vol_score = abs(math.log(vol_ratio))

    angle_penalty = 0.0
    n_angle_constraints = 0

    if crystal_system in ('cubic', 'tetragonal', 'orthorhombic'):
        angle_penalty += abs(alpha - 90.0)
        angle_penalty += abs(beta - 90.0)
        angle_penalty += abs(gamma - 90.0)
        n_angle_constraints = 3
    elif crystal_system == 'hexagonal':
        angle_penalty += abs(alpha - 90.0)
        angle_penalty += abs(beta - 90.0)
        angle_penalty += abs(gamma - 120.0)
        n_angle_constraints = 3
    elif crystal_system == 'trigonal':

        if abs(gamma - 120.0) < abs(gamma - 90.0):
            angle_penalty += abs(alpha - 90.0)
            angle_penalty += abs(beta - 90.0)
            angle_penalty += abs(gamma - 120.0)
            n_angle_constraints = 3
        else:
            angle_penalty += abs(alpha - beta)
            angle_penalty += abs(beta - gamma)
            angle_penalty += abs(alpha - gamma)
            n_angle_constraints = 3
    elif crystal_system == 'monoclinic':

        angle_penalty += abs(alpha - 90.0)
        angle_penalty += abs(gamma - 90.0)
        n_angle_constraints = 2
    elif crystal_system == 'triclinic':
        n_angle_constraints = 0
    else:
        n_angle_constraints = 0

    angle_score = (angle_penalty / n_angle_constraints) if n_angle_constraints > 0 else 0.0

    edge_penalty = 0.0
    n_edge_constraints = 0

    if crystal_system == 'cubic':
        edge_penalty += abs(a - b) / a
        edge_penalty += abs(b - c) / b
        edge_penalty += abs(a - c) / a
        n_edge_constraints = 3
    elif crystal_system == 'tetragonal':
        edge_penalty += abs(a - b) / a
        n_edge_constraints = 1
    elif crystal_system == 'hexagonal':
        edge_penalty += abs(a - b) / a
        n_edge_constraints = 1
    elif crystal_system == 'trigonal':
        if abs(gamma - 120.0) < abs(gamma - 90.0):
            edge_penalty += abs(a - b) / a
            n_edge_constraints = 1
        else:
            edge_penalty += abs(a - b) / a
            edge_penalty += abs(b - c) / b
            edge_penalty += abs(a - c) / a
            n_edge_constraints = 3

    edge_consistency_score = (edge_penalty / n_edge_constraints) if n_edge_constraints > 0 else 0.0

    score = (
        edge_anisotropy
        + vol_score
        + angle_score / 10.0
        + edge_consistency_score * 5.0
    )

    return float(score)
R47 Shortest same-element periodic contact relative to the covalent-radius sum; like-element proximity, not assigned like charge

(Absence of unphysical anion–anion or cation–cation repulsions) Screen all pairwise distances in the CIF for unreasonably close like-charge contacts (e.g., O···O < 2.4 Å without H-bond justification, or metal–metal overlaps). Such steric/electrostatic violations indicate an unrelaxed or fictitious structure that would collapse under thermodynamic equilibrium, making synthesis implausible.

def rule_47(structure):

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shortest_translation(s):
        lat=s.lattice
        upper=min(lat.abc)
        pts=lat.get_points_in_sphere([[0,0,0]],[0,0,0],upper+1e-6)
        return min(float(q[1]) for q in pts if q[1]>1e-8)

    def contacts(s,mode):

        sym=symbols(s)
        rad=[cov(x) for x in sym]
        D=s.distance_matrix
        trans=shortest_translation(s)
        values=[]
        for i in range(len(s)):
            if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[i]).is_metal: continue
            values.append(trans/(2*rad[i]))
            for j in range(i):
                if mode=="same" and sym[i]!=sym[j]: continue
                if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[j]).is_metal: continue
                values.append(float(D[i,j])/(rad[i]+rad[j]))
        return values

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return min(contacts(s,"same"))
R48 Signed local electrostatic contact sum per site from BVS-nearest common valences (half the directed first-shell sum); finite-range proxy, not a long-range Madelung energy

(The Madelung constant and overall lattice electrostatic energy should be negative, reflecting net Coulombic attraction. Structures exhibiting extreme charge segregation, very high local electrostatic potential at any site, or a positive net electrostatic energy are thermodynamically unstable and will resist formation during any synthesis procedure.)

def rule_48(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def nearest_states(s,b):
        mono=len(s.composition.elements)==1
        ref=[]
        for site,obs in zip(s,b):
            z=0.
            for el,occ in site.species.items():
                q=min(vals(el.symbol,mono),key=lambda x:(abs(obs-x),abs(x),x))
                z+=occ*q
            ref.append(z/sum(site.species.values()))
        return np.asarray(ref,dtype=float)

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    def nominal_assignment(s):

        am=s.composition.element_composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        sy=sorted(am)
        if len(sy)==1: return {sy[0]:0}
        xs={k:electr(k) for k in sy}; mean=sum(am[k]*xs[k] for k in sy)/sum(am.values())
        states={0.:(0.,0.,0.,())}
        for k in sy:
            nxt={}
            common=vals(k,False)
            available=tuple(sorted(set(Element(k).oxidation_states)|set(common)))
            for net,(p,u,a,qs) in states.items():
                for q in available:
                    key=round(net+am[k]*q,8)
                    score=(p+am[k]*max(0.,q*(xs[k]-mean)),u+(0. if q in common else am[k]),a+am[k]*abs(q),qs+(q,))
                    if key not in nxt or score<nxt[key]: nxt[key]=score
            states=nxt
        net=min(states,key=lambda k:(abs(k),states[k]))
        return dict(zip(sy,states[net][3]))

    def topological_valence(s):

        assignment=nominal_assignment(s)
        occ=np.asarray([site.species.num_atoms for site in s])
        q=np.asarray([sum(v*assignment[e.symbol] for e,v in site.species.items())/o for site,o in zip(s,occ)])
        incoming=np.zeros(len(s))
        if not (np.any(q>0) and np.any(q<0)): return incoming
        distances=s.distance_matrix
        minima=np.array([np.min(distances[i,np.where(q*qi<0)[0]]) if qi!=0 else 0. for i,qi in enumerate(q)])
        cutoff=1.20*float(max(minima))+1e-6
        ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
        rows=[[] for site in s]
        for i,j,d in zip(ii,jj,dd):
            if q[i]*q[j]<0 and 1e-8<d<=1.20*minima[i]+1e-7: rows[int(i)].append(int(j))
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if not row:
                if q[i]!=0: raise ValueError("Nominal ionic site has no opposite neighbor")
                continue
            den=sum(occ[j] for j in row)
            for j in row: incoming[j]-=q[i]*occ[i]/den
        return incoming

    def bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        if any(el.symbol not in params for el in st.composition.element_composition.elements):
            return topological_valence(st)
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    b=bvs(s);z=nearest_states(s,b)
    return sum(z[i]*z[j]/d for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)) for j,t,d in row)/(2*len(s))
R49 Fraction of adjacent coordination-center pairs sharing two or more first-shell sites (edge/face versus single-vertex sharing)

Cation and anion polyhedra (tetrahedra, octahedra, square-planar, etc.) must exhibit valid vertex-sharing, edge-sharing, or face-sharing connectivity patterns. Edge-sharing of certain tetrahedra (e.g., P–O–P at very small angles) or face-sharing of small-cation octahedra that violate Pauling's rules indicate an unattainable framework topology.

def rule_49(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def shares(s):

        incoming=[[] for site in s]
        for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)):
            for j,img,d in row: incoming[j].append((i,tuple(-v for v in img)))
        pair=Counter()
        for ligand in incoming:
            for (i,a),(j,b) in itertools.combinations(ligand,2):
                t=tuple(y-x for x,y in zip(a,b))
                f=(i,j,t); rev=(j,i,tuple(-x for x in t))
                pair[min(f,rev)]+=1
        return sum(n>=2 for n in pair.values())/len(pair) if pair else 0.

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return shares(s)
R50 RMS valence mismatch on the negatively polarized branch per occupied site; no anionic contribution gives a defined zero

(Electrostatic valence balance per Pauling's third rule: The sum of bond valences arriving at each anion should equal its formal charge.                         sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
