R01 Ratio of the largest to the smallest constituent covalent radius.

(Extreme ionic/covalent radius ratio: r_max / r_min over all constituent elements using tabulated Shannon ionic radii or Cordero covalent radii at the relevant coordination.) A large ratio indicates severe size mismatch that distorts the local coordination polyhedra, generates internal strain, and penalizes the formation energy. Conversely, ratios near ideal close-packing values (0.41–0.73) allow efficient anion packing and stabilize the structure relative to the separated elements.

def rule_01(structure):

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def parts(s):
        comp=s.composition.element_composition
        return [(el,float(n)/comp.num_atoms) for el,n in comp.items()]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    r=[cov(e.symbol) for e,p in parts(s)]
    return max(r)/min(r)
R02 Distinct element count

(Number of distinct elements in the chemical formula) This integer captures compositional complexity and the mixing-entropy-like effect on stability. Binary compounds benefit from two strong, chemically matched sub-lattices; adding a third or fourth element introduces competing bond types, site-disorder penalties, and reduced bond-directionality, often shifting ΔE_form toward less negative values. It is a minimal yet powerful indicator of whether the structure can achieve near-optimal bonding for all constituent pairs.

def rule_02(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return len(s.composition.elements)
R03 Mean bonded electronegativity contrast plus half the elemental range and the sample standard deviation; uses a periodic first shell when CrystalNN returns no contacts.

(Electronegativity difference, Δχ: the maximum or mean Pauling electronegativity gap between cationic and anionic species in the formula. A larger Δχ indicates stronger ionic (Coulombic) bonding, which is the dominant stabilizing contribution to a negative formation energy. This quantity is computed purely from the elemental composition and fixed tabulated Pauling electronegativity values, with no reliance on any computed energy of the material.)

def rule_03(structure):
    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    elements = set(structure.composition.elements)
    chi = {}
    for el in elements:
        e = Element(el)
        x = e.X
        if x is None:
            return float('nan')
        try:
            x = float(x)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return float('nan')
        if math.isnan(x):
            return float('nan')
        chi[el] = x

    try:
        cnn = CrystalNN()
        nn_info = cnn.get_all_nn_info(structure)
    except Exception:
        return float('nan')

    if not any(nn_info):
        nn_info = [[{'site_index':j} for j,t,d in row] for row in shell(structure)]

    bond_dchis = []
    seen_pairs = set()
    n_sites = len(structure)

    for i in range(n_sites):
        site_i = structure[i]
        for nn in nn_info[i]:
            j = nn['site_index']

            pair = (min(i, j), max(i, j))
            if pair in seen_pairs:
                continue
            seen_pairs.add(pair)
            el_a = site_i.specie
            el_b = structure[j].specie
            dchi = abs(chi[el_a] - chi[el_b])
            bond_dchis.append(dchi)

    if not bond_dchis:
        return float('nan')

    n_bonds = len(bond_dchis)
    mean_dchi = sum(bond_dchis) / n_bonds

    if n_bonds > 1:
        var_dchi = sum((d - mean_dchi) ** 2 for d in bond_dchis) / (n_bonds - 1)
        std_dchi = math.sqrt(var_dchi)
    else:
        std_dchi = 0.0

    chi_values = list(chi.values())
    chi_spread = max(chi_values) - min(chi_values)

    scalar = mean_dchi + 0.5 * chi_spread + std_dchi

    return float(scalar)
R04 Fractional bond-length RMS with an electronegativity switch, oxidation-state assignment, Shannon or covalent radii, and a periodic nearest-shell fallback.

Mean nearest-neighbour bond-length deviation from the sum of tabulated ionic (or covalent) radii. A positive deviation signals stretched, under-coordinated bonds (weaker than ideal), while a negative deviation signals compressed, over-repulsive bonds. Both types of strain raise the total energy relative to a perfectly matched reference, directly shifting formation energy per atom. The metric is computed from CIF Cartesian/fractional coordinates plus tabulated Shannon or Cordero radii.

def rule_04(structure):
    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def undirected_cnn(st):
        probe=CrystalNN()
        rows=probe.get_all_nn_info(st)
        geom=None; links={}
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if row: neighbors=[(int(v["site_index"]),tuple(int(round(x)) for x in v["image"])) for v in row]
            else:
                if geom is None: geom=shell(st)
                neighbors=[(j,t) for j,t,d in geom[i]]
            for j,t in neighbors:
                k=min((i,j,t),(j,i,tuple(-x for x in t)))
                if k not in links:
                    links[k]=float(np.linalg.norm(st.lattice.get_cartesian_coords(st[j].frac_coords+np.asarray(t)-st[i].frac_coords)))
        out={i:[] for i in range(len(st))}
        for (i,j,t),d in sorted(links.items()):
            out[i].append({"site_index":j,"image":t,"distance_A":d})
            out[j].append({"site_index":i,"image":tuple(-x for x in t),"distance_A":d})
        return out

    def shannon(el,cn,ox):
        roman={3:"III",4:"IV",5:"V",6:"VI",7:"VII",8:"VIII",12:"XII"}
        opts=Element(el.symbol).data.get("Shannon radii",{}).get(str(int(ox)),{}).get(roman.get(cn),{})
        row=opts.get("")
        if row is None and len(opts)==1: row=list(opts.values())[0]
        return None if row is None else row.get("ionic_radius")
    _cov_table = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
    struct = structure

    try:
        cnn = CrystalNN()
        nn_info = undirected_cnn(struct)
    except Exception:
        return float('nan')

    if not nn_info:
        return float('nan')

    comp = struct.composition
    el_dic = comp.get_el_amt_dict()

    def _assign_ox(el_dic):
        if len(el_dic) == 1:
            return {list(el_dic.keys())[0]: 0}
        fixed, flexible = {}, {}
        for sym, cnt in el_dic.items():
            el = Element(sym)
            Z = el.Z
            if Z == 9:
                fixed[sym] = -1
            elif Z == 8:
                fixed[sym] = -2
            elif Z in (1, 11, 19, 37, 55, 87):
                fixed[sym] = 1
            elif Z in (4, 12, 20, 38, 56, 88):
                fixed[sym] = 2
            elif Z in (13, 31, 49, 81):
                fixed[sym] = 3
            elif Z in (35, 53, 85):
                fixed[sym] = -1
            else:
                flexible[sym] = cnt
        fixed_sum = sum(fixed[s] * el_dic[s] for s in fixed)
        if not flexible:
            return fixed
        if len(flexible) == 1:
            s = list(flexible.keys())[0]
            cnt = el_dic[s]
            return {**fixed, s: round(-fixed_sum / cnt)} if cnt > 0 else fixed

        common = {
            'H': 1, 'C': 4, 'N': -3, 'P': -3, 'As': -3, 'Sb': -3, 'Bi': -3,
            'S': -2, 'Se': -2, 'Te': -2, 'Po': -2,
            'Sc': 3, 'Ti': 4, 'V': 5, 'Cr': 3, 'Mn': 3, 'Fe': 3, 'Co': 3,
            'Ni': 2, 'Cu': 2, 'Zn': 2, 'Ga': 3, 'Ge': 4,
            'Y': 3, 'Zr': 4, 'Nb': 5, 'Mo': 6, 'Tc': 7, 'Ru': 4,
            'Rh': 3, 'Pd': 2, 'Ag': 1, 'Cd': 2, 'In': 3, 'Sn': 4,
            'Hf': 4, 'Ta': 5, 'W': 6, 'Re': 7, 'Os': 4, 'Ir': 4,
            'Pt': 2, 'Au': 3, 'Hg': 2, 'Tl': 3, 'Pb': 2,
            'La': 3, 'Ce': 3, 'Pr': 3, 'Nd': 3, 'Sm': 3, 'Eu': 2, 'Gd': 3,
            'Tb': 3, 'Dy': 3, 'Ho': 3, 'Er': 3, 'Tm': 3, 'Yb': 2, 'Lu': 3,
            'Ac': 3, 'Th': 4, 'Pa': 5, 'U': 6, 'Np': 5, 'Pu': 4,
        }
        result = dict(fixed)
        assigned = 0
        for s in flexible:
            ox = common.get(s, 0)
            result[s] = ox
            assigned += ox * el_dic[s]

        residual = -fixed_sum - assigned
        total_flex = sum(el_dic[s] for s in flexible)
        if total_flex > 0 and abs(residual) > 0.01:
            adj = residual / total_flex
            for s in flexible:
                result[s] = round(result[s] + adj)
        return result

    ox_states = _assign_ox(el_dic)

    coord_nums = {}
    for i, nn_list in nn_info.items():
        coord_nums[i] = len(nn_list)

    valid_shannon_coords = [3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 12]

    def _snap_coord(c):
        if c in valid_shannon_coords:
            return c
        return min(valid_shannon_coords, key=lambda v: abs(v - c))

    deviations = []

    for i, nn_list in nn_info.items():
        site_i = struct[i]
        el_i = site_i.specie
        coord_i = _snap_coord(coord_nums[i])
        ox_i = ox_states.get(el_i.symbol, 0)

        for nn in nn_list:
            j = nn['site_index']
            if j < i or (j == i and tuple(nn['image']) <= (0,0,0)):
                continue
            site_j = struct[j]
            el_j = site_j.specie
            coord_j = _snap_coord(coord_nums[j])
            ox_j = ox_states.get(el_j.symbol, 0)
            d_ij = (float(nn['distance_A']) if 'distance_A' in nn else float(np.linalg.norm(nn['site'].coords - site_i.coords)))

            chi_diff = abs(el_i.X - el_j.X)
            if chi_diff < 0.5:
                r_i = _cov_table.get((el_i).symbol)
                r_j = _cov_table.get((el_j).symbol)
            else:
                try:
                    r_i = shannon(el_i, coord_i, ox_i)
                except Exception:
                    r_i = None
                try:
                    r_j = shannon(el_j, coord_j, ox_j)
                except Exception:
                    r_j = None
                if r_i is None:
                    r_i = _cov_table.get((el_i).symbol)
                if r_j is None:
                    r_j = _cov_table.get((el_j).symbol)

            if r_i is None or r_j is None:
                return float('nan')

            r_sum = r_i + r_j
            if r_sum <= 0.0:
                return float('nan')

            delta = (d_ij - r_sum) / r_sum
            deviations.append(delta)

    if not deviations:
        return float('nan')

    rms = math.sqrt(sum(d * d for d in deviations) / len(deviations))
    return float(rms)
R05 Number of space-group operations in the input setting

Space-group order (number of symmetry operations)** – Read the full space-group symbol from the CIF and look up its point-group and translation order (e.g., Pm3̄m = 48, P1 = 1). Higher symmetry reduces the number of independent positional and strain degrees of freedom, meaning fewer modes of destabilization. Empirically, high-symmetry prototypes (rock-salt, BCC, HCP) occupy lower-energy minima, so the symmetry count is a strong structural prior for formation energy.

def rule_05(structure):
    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(structure)
        ops = sga.get_symmetry_operations()
        return float(len(ops))
    except Exception:
        return float('nan')
R06 Mean number of periodic first-shell neighbors (1.20 times the shortest distance at each site)

Average nearest-neighbor coordination number per atom**: Extracted directly from the CIF by counting symmetry-unique neighbors within a distance cutoff (e.g., 1.2 × mean bond length). Higher average CN implies more bonding interactions per atom, increasing cohesive stabilization and driving the formation energy more negative.

def rule_06(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return np.mean([len(x) for x in shell(s)])
R07 Stoichiometry-weighted contrast of unlike first-neighbor contacts; uses a physical first shell when CrystalNN returns no contacts.

Mean cation–anion electronegativity difference (Pauling scale), weighted over all distinct pairs in the formula. Larger differences favor stronger ionic bond formation, which is a primary driving force for negative DFT formation energies relative to elemental references.

def rule_07(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def undirected_cnn(st):
        probe=CrystalNN()
        rows=probe.get_all_nn_info(st)
        geom=None; links={}
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if row: neighbors=[(int(v["site_index"]),tuple(int(round(x)) for x in v["image"])) for v in row]
            else:
                if geom is None: geom=shell(st)
                neighbors=[(j,t) for j,t,d in geom[i]]
            for j,t in neighbors:
                k=min((i,j,t),(j,i,tuple(-x for x in t)))
                if k not in links:
                    links[k]=float(np.linalg.norm(st.lattice.get_cartesian_coords(st[j].frac_coords+np.asarray(t)-st[i].frac_coords)))
        out={i:[] for i in range(len(st))}
        for (i,j,t),d in sorted(links.items()):
            out[i].append({"site_index":j,"image":t,"distance_A":d})
            out[j].append({"site_index":i,"image":tuple(-x for x in t),"distance_A":d})
        return out

    struct = structure.copy()

    comp = struct.composition
    elements = list(comp.elements)

    chi = {}
    for el in elements:
        try:
            chi[el] = float(el.X)
        except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError):
            return float('nan')

    if len(elements) < 2:
        return 0.0

    try:
        cnn = CrystalNN()
    except Exception:
        return float('nan')

    en_diffs = []
    weights = []

    complete_nn = undirected_cnn(struct)
    n_sites = len(struct)
    for i in range(n_sites):
        try:
            neighbors = [(v['site_index'],v['distance_A']) for v in complete_nn[i]]
        except Exception:
            return float('nan')
        for j, dist in neighbors:
            if i < j:
                el_i = Element(struct[i].specie.symbol)
                el_j = Element(struct[j].specie.symbol)

                if el_i != el_j:
                    d_chi = abs(chi[el_i] - chi[el_j])
                    en_diffs.append(d_chi)

                    w = comp[el_i] * comp[el_j]
                    weights.append(w)

    if not en_diffs:

        return 0.0

    total_w = sum(weights)
    if total_w <= 0.0:
        return float('nan')

    result = sum(d * w for d, w in zip(en_diffs, weights)) / total_w
    return float(result)
R08 Mean second-to-first radial-shell distance ratio for unlike-element contacts (all contacts in elemental crystals); packing proxy without assuming site oxidation states

(Second-neighbor packing ratio)** From the CIF, compute the mean first-neighbor cation–anion distance and the mean second-neighbor cation–anion distance. Their ratio approximates the local packing geometry (close-packed ≈ 1.732 for cubic). Ratios close to ideal close-packing values indicate efficient packing and stronger secondary electrostatic interactions, lowering ΔH_f per atom.

def rule_08(structure):

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def radial_shell_ratio(s):

        species=np.asarray(symbols(s));mono=len(set(species))==1
        cutoff=max(3.,2.1*(s.volume/len(s))**(1/3))
        for trial in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            ratios=[]
            for i in range(len(s)):
                keep=(ii==i)&(dd>1e-8)
                if not mono: keep=keep&(species[jj]!=species[i])
                ds=np.sort(dd[keep])
                if not len(ds): break
                first=ds[ds<=1.20*ds[0]+1e-7];outer=ds[ds>1.20*ds[0]+1e-7]
                if not len(outer) or cutoff<1.05*outer[0]+1e-7: break
                second=outer[outer<=1.05*outer[0]+1e-7]
                ratios.append(np.mean(second)/np.mean(first))
            if len(ratios)==len(s): return np.mean(ratios)
            cutoff*=2
        raise ValueError("Cannot resolve two periodic radial shells")

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return radial_shell_ratio(s)
R09 Symmetry complexity from site-orbit multiplicities, Wyckoff letters, symmetry order, and distinct-element count.

Number of crystallographically unique atomic sites (Wyckoff positions) in the asymmetric unit** – Read directly from the CIF. A larger count of inequivalent sites signals greater structural complexity and more degrees of atomic relaxation, which typically correlates with a less negative (less exothermic) formation energy because the structure is farther from a simple, high-symmetry low-energy motif.

def rule_09(structure):
    struct = structure.copy()

    if len(struct) == 0:
        return float('nan')

    try:
        sga = SpacegroupAnalyzer(struct)

        sym_dataset = sga.get_symmetry_dataset()
        pg_order = len(sym_dataset.rotations)
        if pg_order <= 0:
            return float('nan')

        labels = list(sym_dataset.wyckoffs)
        equiv = list(map(int,sym_dataset.equivalent_atoms))
        sizes = Counter(equiv)
        wyckoff_symbols = [str(sizes[j])+w for j,w in zip(equiv,labels)]
        if len(wyckoff_symbols) != len(struct):
            return float('nan')

        wyckoff_letters = set()
        multiplicities = []
        for ws in wyckoff_symbols:

            letter = ws[-1]
            mult_str = ws[:-1]
            if not mult_str.isdigit():
                return float('nan')
            wyckoff_letters.add(letter)
            multiplicities.append(int(mult_str))

        n_wyckoff = len(wyckoff_letters)

        max_mult = max(multiplicities)
        n_special = sum(1 for m in multiplicities if m < max_mult)
        n_total = len(multiplicities)
        f_special = n_special / n_total

        n_atoms = len(struct)
        ratio_sites = n_wyckoff / n_atoms

        comp = struct.composition
        n_elements = len(comp.elements)

        complexity = ratio_sites * (1.0 / pg_order) * (1.0 - f_special) * n_elements

        return float(complexity)

    except Exception:
        return float('nan')
R10 Variance of periodic first-shell coordination numbers across sites

Coordination-number distribution and its variance across all sites.** Counted directly from CIF neighbor lists within a geometric cutoff. Sites with unusually high or low coordination relative to what is expected from the stoichiometry and ionic sizes indicate frustrated bonding or unsatisfied valence, both of which elevate formation energy. A narrow, chemically rational CN distribution (e.g., all cations octahedrally coordinated in a spinel) signals a well-relaxed structure.

def rule_10(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return np.var([len(x) for x in shell(s)])
R11 Mean first-shell distance divided by its pair's covalent-radius sum

Average nearest-neighbor bond length normalized by the sum of ionic (or covalent) radii** – From CIF interatomic distances and tabulated Shannon/Pauling radii, form d_ij/(r_i + r_j) for every nearest-neighbor pair and average. Ratios significantly below 1 indicate bond compression (stronger overlap, more negative E_f); ratios above 1 signal over-stretched, weaker bonds (less negative E_f). This captures the geometric bond-strength lever directly.

def rule_11(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shellratios(s):
        rad=[cov(x) for x in symbols(s)]
        return [d/(rad[i]+rad[j]) for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)) for j,t,d in row]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return np.mean(shellratios(s))
R12 Stoichiometry-weighted standard deviation of outer-shell valence-electron counts

Valence-electron spread (σ_VEC):** The standard deviation of tabulated valence electron counts across the distinct elements present, weighted by stoichiometry. A large σ_VEC signals strong electron transfer (ionic) or orbital hybridization (p–d) between dissimilar atoms, enhancing bonding contrast beyond simple averaging and typically correlating with more negative formation energies relative to the elemental baseline.

def rule_12(structure):

    def ve(el):
        cfg=el.full_electronic_structure
        n=int(el.row)

        return sum(k for m,orb,k in cfg if (m==n and orb in ("s","p")) or
                   (el.is_transition_metal and m==n-1 and orb=="d"))

    def parts(s):
        comp=s.composition.element_composition
        return [(el,float(n)/comp.num_atoms) for el,n in comp.items()]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    x=[(ve(e),p) for e,p in parts(s)]
    avg=sum(v*p for v,p in x)
    return math.sqrt(sum(p*(v-avg)**2 for v,p in x))
R13 Minimum absolute residual per reduced formula under one common integer valence per element; mixed valence not assigned

Charge-balance residual and oxidation-state pattern.** Assign the most common integer oxidation states consistent with the composition and sum the net charge per formula unit. A residual ≠ 0 signals an ideal charge imbalance that must be compensated by covalent/metallic bonding or defects, increasing formation energy. The number of distinct oxidation states and their cation:anion ratio also capture how well the structure satisfies electrostatic neutrality.

def rule_13(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def spectrum(s):

        amounts=s.composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        result={0.:1}
        for sym,n in sorted(amounts.items()):
            nxt=defaultdict(int)
            for prev,mult in result.items():
                for z in vals(sym,len(amounts)==1):
                    nxt[round(prev+n*z,8)]+=mult
            result=nxt
        return result

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    comp,factor=s.composition.element_composition.get_reduced_composition_and_factor()
    return min(abs(z) for z in spectrum(s))/factor
R14 Mean magnitude of independently selected common valences closest to geometry-derived bond-valence sums; ionicity proxy

The average absolute formal charge per ion, calculated from stoichiometry and standard oxidation states, scales the Coulombic Madelung-energy contribution. Higher charges (e.g., +3/−3 versus +1/−1) produce much stronger electrostatic stabilization, making formation energy substantially more negative, assuming comparable interionic distances.

def rule_14(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def nearest_states(s,b):
        mono=len(s.composition.elements)==1
        ref=[]
        for site,obs in zip(s,b):
            z=0.
            for el,occ in site.species.items():
                q=min(vals(el.symbol,mono),key=lambda x:(abs(obs-x),abs(x),x))
                z+=occ*q
            ref.append(z/sum(site.species.values()))
        return np.asarray(ref,dtype=float)

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    def nominal_assignment(s):

        am=s.composition.element_composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        sy=sorted(am)
        if len(sy)==1: return {sy[0]:0}
        xs={k:electr(k) for k in sy}; mean=sum(am[k]*xs[k] for k in sy)/sum(am.values())
        states={0.:(0.,0.,0.,())}
        for k in sy:
            nxt={}
            common=vals(k,False)
            available=tuple(sorted(set(Element(k).oxidation_states)|set(common)))
            for net,(p,u,a,qs) in states.items():
                for q in available:
                    key=round(net+am[k]*q,8)
                    score=(p+am[k]*max(0.,q*(xs[k]-mean)),u+(0. if q in common else am[k]),a+am[k]*abs(q),qs+(q,))
                    if key not in nxt or score<nxt[key]: nxt[key]=score
            states=nxt
        net=min(states,key=lambda k:(abs(k),states[k]))
        return dict(zip(sy,states[net][3]))

    def topological_valence(s):

        assignment=nominal_assignment(s)
        occ=np.asarray([site.species.num_atoms for site in s])
        q=np.asarray([sum(v*assignment[e.symbol] for e,v in site.species.items())/o for site,o in zip(s,occ)])
        incoming=np.zeros(len(s))
        if not (np.any(q>0) and np.any(q<0)): return incoming
        distances=s.distance_matrix
        minima=np.array([np.min(distances[i,np.where(q*qi<0)[0]]) if qi!=0 else 0. for i,qi in enumerate(q)])
        cutoff=1.20*float(max(minima))+1e-6
        ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
        rows=[[] for site in s]
        for i,j,d in zip(ii,jj,dd):
            if q[i]*q[j]<0 and 1e-8<d<=1.20*minima[i]+1e-7: rows[int(i)].append(int(j))
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if not row:
                if q[i]!=0: raise ValueError("Nominal ionic site has no opposite neighbor")
                continue
            den=sum(occ[j] for j in row)
            for j in row: incoming[j]-=q[i]*occ[i]/den
        return incoming

    def bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        if any(el.symbol not in params for el in st.composition.element_composition.elements):
            return topological_valence(st)
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    b=bvs(s);z=nearest_states(s,b)
    return np.mean(np.abs(z))
R15 Stoichiometry-weighted group-based valence convention

Average valence-electron count per atom in the formula unit (sum of tabulated group valences divided by total atom count). This governs whether bonding is predominantly ionic, covalent, or metallic; intermediate electron counts that allow filled-shell or band-gap stabilization tend to lower formation energy relative to very electron-rich or electron-poor compositions.

def rule_15(structure):

    comp = structure.composition
    total_atoms = sum(comp.values())

    if total_atoms == 0:
        return float('nan')

    def _valence_electrons(el):

        Z = el.Z

        if 57 <= Z <= 71 or 89 <= Z <= 103:
            return 3.0
        group = el.group
        if group is None:
            return float('nan')

        if 1 <= group <= 2:
            return float(group)

        if 3 <= group <= 12:
            return float(group)

        if 13 <= group <= 18:
            return float(group - 10)
        return float('nan')

    total_valence = 0.0
    for el, count in comp.items():
        ve = _valence_electrons(el)
        if math.isnan(ve):
            return float('nan')
        total_valence += ve * count

    return total_valence / float(total_atoms)
R16 Continuous per-site angular anisotropy (mean squared first-shell directional resultant); distortion proxy, not a literal variance about one ideal shape

(Variance of nearest-neighbour bond angles from the ideal angle of the dominant coordination polyhedron—109.47° for tetrahedral, 90°/180° for octahedral, 70.53° for trigonal-bipyramidal, etc.—computed from CIF coordinates.) Angular distortion breaks degeneracies in the local crystal-field and hybridisation manifold, populating higher-energy antibonding orbitals and introducing torsional strain. Larger variance therefore signals a less optimal electronic structure and a higher (less exothermic) formation energy per atom.

def rule_16(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    vals_site=[]
    for i,row in enumerate(shell(s)):
        unit=np.array([s.lattice.get_cartesian_coords(s[j].frac_coords+np.asarray(t)-s[i].frac_coords)/d for j,t,d in row])
        v=np.mean(unit,axis=0)
        vals_site.append(float(np.dot(v,v)))
    return np.mean(vals_site)
R17 RMS distance of site bond-valence sum to a chemically allowed common oxidation state; estimated, not supplied formal valences

The root-mean-square deviation of bond-valence sums from the target integer valence (using Brown–Altermatt or BVS parameters, computed from CIF bond lengths + tabulated b and R₀ constants) measures local electronic over- or under-bonding. Atoms whose Σs(r) departs significantly from their formal oxidation state carry residual charge frustration, indicating an electronically unsatisfied environment that destabilizes the crystal relative to an ideal valence-satisfied reference.

def rule_17(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def nearest_states(s,b):
        mono=len(s.composition.elements)==1
        ref=[]
        for site,obs in zip(s,b):
            z=0.
            for el,occ in site.species.items():
                q=min(vals(el.symbol,mono),key=lambda x:(abs(obs-x),abs(x),x))
                z+=occ*q
            ref.append(z/sum(site.species.values()))
        return np.asarray(ref,dtype=float)

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    def nominal_assignment(s):

        am=s.composition.element_composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        sy=sorted(am)
        if len(sy)==1: return {sy[0]:0}
        xs={k:electr(k) for k in sy}; mean=sum(am[k]*xs[k] for k in sy)/sum(am.values())
        states={0.:(0.,0.,0.,())}
        for k in sy:
            nxt={}
            common=vals(k,False)
            available=tuple(sorted(set(Element(k).oxidation_states)|set(common)))
            for net,(p,u,a,qs) in states.items():
                for q in available:
                    key=round(net+am[k]*q,8)
                    score=(p+am[k]*max(0.,q*(xs[k]-mean)),u+(0. if q in common else am[k]),a+am[k]*abs(q),qs+(q,))
                    if key not in nxt or score<nxt[key]: nxt[key]=score
            states=nxt
        net=min(states,key=lambda k:(abs(k),states[k]))
        return dict(zip(sy,states[net][3]))

    def topological_valence(s):

        assignment=nominal_assignment(s)
        occ=np.asarray([site.species.num_atoms for site in s])
        q=np.asarray([sum(v*assignment[e.symbol] for e,v in site.species.items())/o for site,o in zip(s,occ)])
        incoming=np.zeros(len(s))
        if not (np.any(q>0) and np.any(q<0)): return incoming
        distances=s.distance_matrix
        minima=np.array([np.min(distances[i,np.where(q*qi<0)[0]]) if qi!=0 else 0. for i,qi in enumerate(q)])
        cutoff=1.20*float(max(minima))+1e-6
        ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
        rows=[[] for site in s]
        for i,j,d in zip(ii,jj,dd):
            if q[i]*q[j]<0 and 1e-8<d<=1.20*minima[i]+1e-7: rows[int(i)].append(int(j))
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if not row:
                if q[i]!=0: raise ValueError("Nominal ionic site has no opposite neighbor")
                continue
            den=sum(occ[j] for j in row)
            for j in row: incoming[j]-=q[i]*occ[i]/den
        return incoming

    def bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        if any(el.symbol not in params for el in st.composition.element_composition.elements):
            return topological_valence(st)
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    b=np.asarray(bvs(s));z=nearest_states(s,b)
    return np.sqrt(np.mean((b-z)**2))
R18 Cell volume divided by the nominal additive covalent-sphere volume

Unit-cell volume per atom (V_cell/N_formula): the per-atom volume extracted directly from CIF lattice parameters and stoichiometry. Smaller volume per atom implies denser packing, shorter average interatomic distances, and stronger collective bonding, all driving more negative formation energies. It also proxies structural compression relative to the constituent elements' own equilibrium molar volumes.

def rule_18(structure):

    struct = structure.copy()

    volume = struct.lattice.volume
    if volume is None or volume <= 0.0:
        return float('nan')

    n_atoms = len(struct)
    if n_atoms == 0:
        return float('nan')

    elem_counts = Counter()
    for site in struct:
        elem_counts[site.specie.symbol] += 1

    total_atomic_volume = 0.0
    for elem_symbol, count in elem_counts.items():
        elem = Element(elem_symbol)
        r_cov = elem.atomic_radius
        if r_cov is None or r_cov <= 0.0:
            return float('nan')
        total_atomic_volume += count * (4.0 / 3.0) * math.pi * r_cov ** 3

    if total_atomic_volume <= 0.0:
        return float('nan')

    packing_ratio = volume / total_atomic_volume

    return float(packing_ratio)
R19 Number of ground-state d electrons on transition metals per reduced formula; absence of transition metals contributes zero

Total d-electron count per transition-metal site (or per formula unit), derived from composition and tabulated ground-state electronic configurations. Partially filled d-shells enable strong directional and exchange-stabilized bonding; d⁰, d⁵ (half-filled), and d¹⁰ configurations often mark local stability maxima in formation energy.

def rule_19(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    comp,factor=s.composition.element_composition.get_reduced_composition_and_factor()
    out=0.
    for el,n in comp.items():
        if not el.is_transition_metal: continue
        shells=el.full_electronic_structure
        states=[(m,k) for m,o,k in shells if o=="d"]
        if states: out+=n*max(states)[1]
    return out
R20 Mean absolute distance of site bond-valence sum to the nearest common nominal valence; not a measured charge

Deviation of the Brown bond-valence sum (Σ exp[−(d−d₀)/b] over all bonds to each atom) from the expected formal oxidation-state valence, averaged over the unit cell (CIF bond lengths + tabulated b, d₀ parameters). Large deviations signal poor charge satisfaction and weaker net bonding, indicating higher formation energy.

def rule_20(structure):

    def vals(sym,mono):
        if mono: return (0,)
        e=Element(sym)
        z=e.common_oxidation_states or e.oxidation_states
        if not z: raise ValueError("No tabulated nominal charge: "+sym)
        return tuple(int(q) for q in z)

    def nearest_states(s,b):
        mono=len(s.composition.elements)==1
        ref=[]
        for site,obs in zip(s,b):
            z=0.
            for el,occ in site.species.items():
                q=min(vals(el.symbol,mono),key=lambda x:(abs(obs-x),abs(x),x))
                z+=occ*q
            ref.append(z/sum(site.species.values()))
        return np.asarray(ref,dtype=float)

    def electr(sym):
        x=float(Element(sym).X)
        if not np.isfinite(x): raise ValueError("No Pauling electronegativity: "+sym)
        return x

    def nominal_assignment(s):

        am=s.composition.element_composition.get_el_amt_dict()
        sy=sorted(am)
        if len(sy)==1: return {sy[0]:0}
        xs={k:electr(k) for k in sy}; mean=sum(am[k]*xs[k] for k in sy)/sum(am.values())
        states={0.:(0.,0.,0.,())}
        for k in sy:
            nxt={}
            common=vals(k,False)
            available=tuple(sorted(set(Element(k).oxidation_states)|set(common)))
            for net,(p,u,a,qs) in states.items():
                for q in available:
                    key=round(net+am[k]*q,8)
                    score=(p+am[k]*max(0.,q*(xs[k]-mean)),u+(0. if q in common else am[k]),a+am[k]*abs(q),qs+(q,))
                    if key not in nxt or score<nxt[key]: nxt[key]=score
            states=nxt
        net=min(states,key=lambda k:(abs(k),states[k]))
        return dict(zip(sy,states[net][3]))

    def topological_valence(s):

        assignment=nominal_assignment(s)
        occ=np.asarray([site.species.num_atoms for site in s])
        q=np.asarray([sum(v*assignment[e.symbol] for e,v in site.species.items())/o for site,o in zip(s,occ)])
        incoming=np.zeros(len(s))
        if not (np.any(q>0) and np.any(q<0)): return incoming
        distances=s.distance_matrix
        minima=np.array([np.min(distances[i,np.where(q*qi<0)[0]]) if qi!=0 else 0. for i,qi in enumerate(q)])
        cutoff=1.20*float(max(minima))+1e-6
        ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
        rows=[[] for site in s]
        for i,j,d in zip(ii,jj,dd):
            if q[i]*q[j]<0 and 1e-8<d<=1.20*minima[i]+1e-7: rows[int(i)].append(int(j))
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            if not row:
                if q[i]!=0: raise ValueError("Nominal ionic site has no opposite neighbor")
                continue
            den=sum(occ[j] for j in row)
            for j in row: incoming[j]-=q[i]*occ[i]/den
        return incoming

    def bvs(st):

        params = {'Ag': [1.12, 1.42], 'Al': [1.13, 1.47], 'As': [1.21, 2.23], 'B': [0.79, 1.6], 'Ba': [1.88, 0.97], 'Be': [0.81, 1.47], 'Bi': [1.54, 1.67], 'Br': [1.13, 2.58], 'C': [0.78, 2.0], 'Ca': [1.5, 1.04], 'Cd': [1.28, 1.46], 'Ce': [1.68, 1.08], 'Cl': [0.99, 2.74], 'Co': [1.09, 1.7], 'Cr': [1.16, 1.56], 'Cs': [2.05, 0.86], 'Cu': [0.87, 1.75], 'Dy': [1.54, 1.1], 'Er': [1.51, 1.11], 'Eu': [1.62, 1.01], 'F': [0.58, 3.98], 'Fe': [1.16, 1.64], 'Ga': [1.14, 1.82], 'Gd': [1.58, 1.11], 'Ge': [1.21, 1.51], 'H': [0.38, 0.89], 'Hf': [1.42, 1.23], 'Hg': [1.32, 1.44], 'Ho': [1.53, 1.1], 'I': [1.33, 2.38], 'In': [1.34, 1.49], 'Ir': [1.37, 1.55], 'K': [1.73, 0.91], 'La': [1.71, 1.08], 'Li': [1.0, 0.97], 'Lu': [1.47, 1.14], 'Mg': [1.21, 1.23], 'Mn': [1.17, 1.6], 'Mo': [1.37, 1.3], 'N': [0.72, 2.61], 'Na': [1.36, 1.01], 'Nb': [1.4, 1.23], 'Nd': [1.64, 1.07], 'Ni': [1.04, 1.75], 'O': [0.63, 3.15], 'P': [1.09, 1.96], 'Pb': [1.53, 1.55], 'Pd': [1.11, 1.35], 'Pr': [1.66, 1.07], 'Rb': [1.84, 0.89], 'Re': [1.37, 1.46], 'Rh': [1.18, 1.54], 'Ru': [1.21, 1.42], 'S': [1.03, 2.35], 'Sb': [1.41, 1.72], 'Sc': [1.34, 1.2], 'Se': [1.18, 2.51], 'Si': [1.12, 1.58], 'Sm': [1.61, 1.07], 'Sn': [1.37, 1.72], 'Sr': [1.66, 0.99], 'Ta': [1.39, 1.33], 'Tb': [1.56, 1.1], 'Te': [1.4, 2.72], 'Th': [1.7, 1.11], 'Ti': [1.27, 1.32], 'Tl': [1.62, 1.44], 'Tm': [1.5, 1.11], 'U': [1.59, 1.22], 'V': [1.21, 1.45], 'W': [1.38, 1.4], 'Y': [1.52, 1.11], 'Yb': [1.49, 1.06], 'Zn': [1.07, 1.66], 'Zr': [1.43, 1.22]}
        en = ('As', 'B', 'Br', 'C', 'Cl', 'F', 'H', 'I', 'N', 'O', 'P', 'S', 'Sb', 'Se', 'Si', 'Te')
        if any(el.symbol not in params for el in st.composition.element_composition.elements):
            return topological_valence(st)
        totals = []
        for centre in st:
            near = st.get_neighbors(centre, 4)
            total = 0.0
            for sp1, occ1 in centre.species.items():
                a = sp1.symbol
                for nb in near:
                    for sp2, occ2 in nb.species.items():
                        b = sp2.symbol
                        if a == b or (a not in en and b not in en):
                            continue
                        r1, c1 = params[a]
                        r2, c2 = params[b]
                        ref = r1 + r2 - r1*r2*(math.sqrt(c1)-math.sqrt(c2))**2/(c1*r1+c2*r2)
                        sign = 1 if Element(a).X < Element(b).X else -1
                        total += occ1*occ2*math.exp((ref - nb.nn_distance*1.015)/0.31)*sign
            totals.append(float(total))
        return totals

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    b=np.asarray(bvs(s))
    return np.mean(np.abs(b-nearest_states(s,b)))
R21 Coefficient of variation of first-shell bond lengths

Bond-length dispersion: From the CIF, extract all nearest-neighbour cation–anion (or metal–metal) distances, then compute the standard deviation normalised by the mean. Low dispersion reflects a uniform, strain-free bonding network (lower formation energy); high dispersion flags compressed or stretched bonds, local over-/under-coordination, and elastic energy stored in the lattice.

def rule_21(structure):

    def shell(s):

        n=len(s)
        if not n: raise ValueError("Empty structure")
        cutoff=max(2.0,1.5*(s.volume/n)**(1/3))
        for attempt in range(7):
            ii,jj,images,dd=s.get_neighbor_list(cutoff)
            mins=np.full(n,np.inf)
            use=dd>1e-8
            np.minimum.at(mins,ii[use],dd[use])
            if np.all(1.2*mins+1e-7<=cutoff): break
            cutoff*=2
        else: raise ValueError("Could not enclose complete periodic first shells")
        out=[[] for i in range(n)]
        for i,j,image,d in zip(ii,jj,images,dd):
            if d>1e-8 and d<=1.2*mins[i]+1e-7:
                out[int(i)].append((int(j),tuple(map(int,image)),float(d)))
        return out

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    d=[d for row in shell(s) for j,t,d in row]
    return np.std(d)/np.mean(d)
R22 Stoichiometry-weighted standard deviation of covalent radii, in angstrom

Variance (or standard deviation) of tabulated Shannon ionic or covalent radii among all constituent elements. A large radius spread indicates severe local size mismatch, generating lattice strain that raises the formation energy above the elemental reference. This is obtained purely from composition and fixed ionic-radius tables.

def rule_22(structure):

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def parts(s):
        comp=s.composition.element_composition
        return [(el,float(n)/comp.num_atoms) for el,n in comp.items()]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    x=[(cov(e.symbol),p) for e,p in parts(s)]
    avg=sum(v*p for v,p in x)
    return math.sqrt(sum(p*(v-avg)**2 for v,p in x))
R23 Strength of the closest metal–metal contact (covalent-radius sum / periodic separation); no metal contacts means zero, not an inferred cation charge

Shortest cation–cation distance**: Extracted from the CIF interatomic-distance list, the minimum distance between like-charged cation species indicates whether metal–metal bonding or Pauli repulsion is operative. Distances below the sum of metallic radii suggest stabilising d–d overlap; excessively short distances signal destabilising electron repulsion.

def rule_23(structure):

    def symbols(s):

        return [max(site.species.items(),key=lambda x:(x[1],x[0].symbol))[0].symbol for site in s]

    def cov(sym):
        fixed = {'H': 0.31, 'He': 0.28, 'Li': 1.28, 'Be': 0.96, 'B': 0.84, 'C': 0.73, 'N': 0.71, 'O': 0.66, 'F': 0.57, 'Ne': 0.58, 'Na': 1.66, 'Mg': 1.41, 'Al': 1.21, 'Si': 1.11, 'P': 1.07, 'S': 1.05, 'Cl': 1.02, 'Ar': 1.06, 'K': 2.03, 'Ca': 1.76, 'Sc': 1.7, 'Ti': 1.6, 'V': 1.53, 'Cr': 1.39, 'Mn': 1.5, 'Fe': 1.42, 'Co': 1.38, 'Ni': 1.24, 'Cu': 1.32, 'Zn': 1.22, 'Ga': 1.22, 'Ge': 1.2, 'As': 1.19, 'Se': 1.2, 'Br': 1.2, 'Kr': 1.16, 'Rb': 2.2, 'Sr': 1.95, 'Y': 1.9, 'Zr': 1.75, 'Nb': 1.64, 'Mo': 1.54, 'Tc': 1.47, 'Ru': 1.46, 'Rh': 1.42, 'Pd': 1.39, 'Ag': 1.45, 'Cd': 1.44, 'In': 1.42, 'Sn': 1.39, 'Sb': 1.39, 'Te': 1.38, 'I': 1.39, 'Xe': 1.4, 'Cs': 2.44, 'Ba': 2.15, 'La': 2.07, 'Ce': 2.04, 'Pr': 2.03, 'Nd': 2.01, 'Pm': 1.99, 'Sm': 1.98, 'Eu': 1.98, 'Gd': 1.96, 'Tb': 1.94, 'Dy': 1.92, 'Ho': 1.92, 'Er': 1.89, 'Tm': 1.9, 'Yb': 1.87, 'Lu': 1.87, 'Hf': 1.75, 'Ta': 1.7, 'W': 1.62, 'Re': 1.51, 'Os': 1.44, 'Ir': 1.41, 'Pt': 1.36, 'Au': 1.36, 'Hg': 1.32, 'Tl': 1.45, 'Pb': 1.46, 'Bi': 1.48, 'Po': 1.4, 'At': 1.5, 'Rn': 1.5, 'Fr': 2.6, 'Ra': 2.21, 'Ac': 2.15, 'Th': 2.06, 'Pa': 2.0, 'U': 1.96, 'Np': 1.9, 'Pu': 1.87, 'Am': 1.8, 'Cm': 1.69}
        if sym not in fixed: raise ValueError("No tabulated covalent radius: "+sym)
        return fixed[sym]

    def shortest_translation(s):
        lat=s.lattice
        upper=min(lat.abc)
        pts=lat.get_points_in_sphere([[0,0,0]],[0,0,0],upper+1e-6)
        return min(float(q[1]) for q in pts if q[1]>1e-8)

    def contacts(s,mode):

        sym=symbols(s)
        rad=[cov(x) for x in sym]
        D=s.distance_matrix
        trans=shortest_translation(s)
        values=[]
        for i in range(len(s)):
            if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[i]).is_metal: continue
            values.append(trans/(2*rad[i]))
            for j in range(i):
                if mode=="same" and sym[i]!=sym[j]: continue
                if mode=="metal" and not Element(sym[j]).is_metal: continue
                values.append(float(D[i,j])/(rad[i]+rad[j]))
        return values

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    q=contacts(s,"metal")
    if not q: return 0.
    if min(q)<=0.: return float("nan")
    return 1./min(q)
R24 Number density of occupied atoms per cubic angstrom

Atomic packing density, defined as the total number of atoms per unit-cell volume (n/V_cell), directly from the CIF. Higher density implies a greater number of interatomic bonding contacts per volume and a larger total cohesive contribution, pushing the formation energy per atom more negative.

def rule_24(structure):

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return s.composition.num_atoms/s.volume
R25 Composition-weighted mean atomic number

Mean atomic number / electron count of the compound:** Compute the composition-weighted average atomic number (or total core-electron count) using tabulated Z values for each element. Heavier, more polarizable elements exhibit more diffuse orbitals, weaker directional bonding, and lower cohesive energies per bond, which systematically shifts the formation energy per atom toward less negative values compared to light-element analogs.

def rule_25(structure):

    def parts(s):
        comp=s.composition.element_composition
        return [(el,float(n)/comp.num_atoms) for el,n in comp.items()]

    s=structure
    if not len(s): return float("nan")
    return sum(e.Z*p for e,p in parts(s))
